&lt;script&gt;
(function() {
  // Collect all devicemotion listeners registered by game code
  var deviceMotionListeners = [];

  // Save original addEventListener
  var originalAddEventListener = window.addEventListener;

  // Intercept addEventListener to capture devicemotion listeners
  window.addEventListener = function(type, listener, options) {
    if (type === 'devicemotion' &amp;&amp; typeof listener === 'function') {
      deviceMotionListeners.push(listener);
      // Also add to native in case permission is granted elsewhere
      return originalAddEventListener.call(this, type, listener, options);
    }
    return originalAddEventListener.call(this, type, listener, options);
  };

  // Also intercept removeEventListener
  var originalRemoveEventListener = window.removeEventListener;
  window.removeEventListener = function(type, listener, options) {
    if (type === 'devicemotion' &amp;&amp; typeof listener === 'function') {
      var index = deviceMotionListeners.indexOf(listener);
      if (index !== -1) {
        deviceMotionListeners.splice(index, 1);
      }
    }
    return originalRemoveEventListener.call(this, type, listener, options);
  };

  // Listen for forwarded motion data from parent
  window.addEventListener('message', function(event) {
    if (!event.data || event.data.type !== '__sekai_device_motion__') return;

    var data = event.data;

    // Create a synthetic event-like object
    var syntheticEvent = {
      accelerationIncludingGravity: data.accelerationIncludingGravity,
      acceleration: data.acceleration,
      rotationRate: data.rotationRate,
      interval: data.interval || 16,
      // Make it look like a real event
      type: 'devicemotion',
      timeStamp: data.timestamp || Date.now(),
      target: window,
      currentTarget: window,
      preventDefault: function() {},
      stopPropagation: function() {},
      stopImmediatePropagation: function() {}
    };

    // Trigger all registered listeners
    deviceMotionListeners.forEach(function(listener) {
      try {
        listener(syntheticEvent);
      } catch (e) {
        // Ignore listener errors
      }
    });
  });
})();
&lt;/script&gt;

&lt;script&gt;
(function() {
  // Track all AudioContexts created in this iframe
  var audioContexts = [];
  var originalAudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;

  if (originalAudioContext) {
    // Patch AudioContext constructor to track instances and enforce initial mute state.
    // If isMuted is true when the context is created, suspend it immediately so that
    // AudioNode sources cannot produce audio before the parent sends receive_audio_mute.
    // This closes the race window between iframe load start and the first mute message.
    var PatchedAudioContext = function() {
      var ctx = new originalAudioContext();
      audioContexts.push(ctx);
      // Per-ctx diagnostic flags. Each AudioContext instance warns at most
      // once for each of the two diagnostics below, so a rAF-driven
      // resume() loop (observed in real games) does not spam vconsole,
      // while new contexts still act as fresh sentinels.
      var resumeBlockWarned = false;
      var browserResumeWarned = false;
      if (typeof ctx.resume === 'function') {
        var originalCtxResume = ctx.resume.bind(ctx);
        ctx.resume = function() {
          if (isMuted) {
            // Game code attempted to resume AudioContext during mute window.
            // Blocked here prevents the audibility observer from reporting a
            // false playing_started. Logged once per ctx to confirm the
            // "game code calls resume()" leak path is real.
            if (!resumeBlockWarned) {
              resumeBlockWarned = true;
              try {
                console.warn(
                  '[sekai-iframe-audio] AudioContext.resume() blocked while muted (once per ctx)'
                );
              } catch (e) {}
            }
            return Promise.resolve();
          }
          return originalCtxResume();
        };
      }
      if (isMuted) {
        ctx.suspend().catch(function() {});
      }
      // Feed perf audibility observer: re-evaluate whenever the context
      // transitions between suspended/running/closed. ctx.suspend()/resume()
      // resolve asynchronously, so the statechange event is the only reliable
      // signal that the context has actually moved.
      try {
        ctx.addEventListener('statechange', function() {
          // Sentinel: resume() is blocked above while muted, so if the ctx
          // still enters 'running' during mute, the only remaining cause is
          // browser-initiated state recovery (e.g. iOS audio session
          // interruption ending). If this log ever appears in practice, the
          // resume() block alone is not enough — we must also guard
          // notifyAudibilityChange() to suppress audible=true while muted.
          // Logged once per ctx to avoid repeated warnings on oscillating
          // state transitions.
          if (ctx.state === 'running' &amp;&amp; isMuted &amp;&amp; !browserResumeWarned) {
            browserResumeWarned = true;
            try {
              console.warn(
                '[sekai-iframe-audio] AudioContext entered running while muted (browser-initiated, once per ctx)'
              );
            } catch (e) {}
          }
          scheduleAudibilityCheck();
        });
      } catch (e) {
        // Ignore listener attach failures on non-conforming contexts
      }
      return ctx;
    };
    PatchedAudioContext.prototype = originalAudioContext.prototype;
    window.AudioContext = PatchedAudioContext;
    if (window.webkitAudioContext) {
      window.webkitAudioContext = PatchedAudioContext;
    }
  }

  // Track all Audio objects (including those created via new Audio())
  var audioElements = [];
  var originalAudio = window.Audio;
  
  function rememberMutedState(el) {
    if (!el) return;
    try {
      if (!mutedElements.has(el)) {
        mutedElements.set(el, !!el.muted);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // Ignore muted state snapshot failures
    }
  }

  if (originalAudio) {
    window.Audio = function(src) {
      var audio = new originalAudio(src);
      audioElements.push(audio);
      if (isMuted) {
        rememberMutedState(audio);
        try { audio.muted = true; } catch (e) {}
      }
      return audio;
    };
    // Preserve prototype chain
    window.Audio.prototype = originalAudio.prototype;
  }

  // Track video elements created via createElement
  var videoElements = [];
  var originalCreateElement = document.createElement;

  document.createElement = function(tagName) {
    var element = originalCreateElement.call(document, tagName);

    // Track audio and video elements
    if (typeof tagName === 'string') {
      var tag = tagName.toLowerCase();
      if (tag === 'audio') {
        audioElements.push(element);
        if (isMuted) {
          rememberMutedState(element);
          try { element.muted = true; } catch (e) {}
        }
      } else if (tag === 'video') {
        videoElements.push(element);
        if (isMuted) {
          rememberMutedState(element);
          try { element.muted = true; } catch (e) {}
        }
      }
    }

    return element;
  };

  // Track elements that are INTENDED to be playing (based on .play()/.pause() calls).
  // This is more reliable than checking .paused at mute time, because iOS pauses
  // audio elements when the app goes to background BEFORE our JS visibility handler
  // runs — by the time muteAllAudio() is called, all elements are already paused
  // by the OS and .paused === true for all of them.
  var intendedPlaying = new Set();

  if (window.HTMLMediaElement) {
    var originalHTMLPlay = HTMLMediaElement.prototype.play;
    var originalHTMLPause = HTMLMediaElement.prototype.pause;
    var internalPause = false;

    HTMLMediaElement.prototype.play = function() {
      intendedPlaying.add(this);
      if (isMuted) {
        rememberMutedState(this);
        try { this.muted = true; } catch (e) {}
        try {
          if (!this.paused) {
            internalPause = true;
            originalHTMLPause.call(this);
          }
        } catch (e) {
          // Ignore pause failures while muted
        } finally {
          internalPause = false;
        }
        return Promise.resolve();
      }
      return originalHTMLPlay.apply(this, arguments);
    };

    HTMLMediaElement.prototype.pause = function() {
      if (!internalPause) {
        intendedPlaying.delete(this);
      }
      return originalHTMLPause.apply(this, arguments);
    };
  }

  // Track elements queued for playback resume — retried on each user touchstart
  // until all elements are playing again. More reliable than { once: true } because
  // it survives cases where the first touch fires before unmuteAllAudio() processes
  // the message (postMessage is async; user may touch the moment they switch back).
  var pendingResume = [];

  // Track muted state to restore correctly
  var mutedElements = new WeakMap();
  var isMuted = false;

  function collectTrackedMediaElements() {
    var elements = [];
    var seen = new Set();

    function pushElement(el) {
      if (!el || seen.has(el)) return;
      seen.add(el);
      elements.push(el);
    }

    document.querySelectorAll('audio, video').forEach(pushElement);
    audioElements.forEach(pushElement);
    videoElements.forEach(pushElement);

    return elements;
  }

  function pauseElementForMute(el) {
    if (!el) return;

    if (!el.paused &amp;&amp; !el.ended) {
      intendedPlaying.add(el);
    }

    try {
      internalPause = true;
      el.pause();
    } finally {
      internalPause = false;
    }
  }

  function lockElementAudio(el) {
    if (!el) return;
    rememberMutedState(el);
    pauseElementForMute(el);
    try {
      el.muted = true;
    } catch (e) {
      // Ignore mute failures for stale or invalid elements
    }
  }

  function muteAllAudio() {
    if (isMuted) return;
    isMuted = true;
    pendingResume = []; // Clear queued resumes — new mute cycle resets state

    // Suspend all AudioContexts
    audioContexts.forEach(function(ctx) {
      if (ctx &amp;&amp; ctx.state === 'running') {
        ctx.suspend().catch(function() {});
      }
    });

    // Pause first, then force mute so background playback cannot leak audio.
    // The audibility observer below will automatically emit
    // sekai_iframe_audio_playing_stopped once the pause/mute propagates, so
    // we no longer post a separate sekai_iframe_audio_muted confirmation.
    collectTrackedMediaElements().forEach(function(el) {
      try {
        lockElementAudio(el);
      } catch (e) {
        // Element might be stale or invalid
      }
    });
  }

  function resumeAllContexts() {
    audioContexts.forEach(function(ctx) {
      // Call resume() on any non-closed context, including 'running'.
      // Reason: muteAllAudio() calls ctx.suspend(), which is async. If
      // unmuteAllAudio() (or a touchstart retry) runs before suspend() resolves,
      // ctx.state is still 'running' — the old suspended/interrupted-only guard
      // would skip it, then suspend() resolves and the context stays permanently
      // suspended. By calling resume() on 'running' contexts too, we queue a
      // resume after the pending suspend, so both operations execute in order
      // (suspend → resume) and the context ends up running as intended.
      // resume() on an already-running context is a no-op per the Web Audio spec.
      if (ctx &amp;&amp; ctx.state !== 'closed') {
        ctx.resume().catch(function() {});
      }
    });
  }

  function unmuteAllAudio() {
    if (!isMuted) return;
    isMuted = false;

    // Resume all AudioContexts immediately (handles 'suspended' and 'interrupted')
    resumeAllContexts();

    // Restore tracked media elements to their original muted state.
    collectTrackedMediaElements().forEach(function(el) {
      var wasMuted = mutedElements.get(el);
      if (wasMuted !== undefined) {
        el.muted = wasMuted;
      } else {
        el.muted = false;
      }
    });
    mutedElements = new WeakMap();

    // Queue intended elements for playback resume.
    // iOS pauses audio/video elements on app-switch before our JS handler runs,
    // so simply unmuting is not enough — we must call .play() to restore.
    // We populate pendingResume here; the persistent touchstart listener below
    // retries on every user touch until all elements are playing again.
    pendingResume = [];
    intendedPlaying.forEach(function(el) {
      if (!el || el.ended) return;
      pendingResume.push(el);
    });
    // Also attempt immediately — succeeds on iOS versions / audio session states
    // that allow .play() without a fresh user gesture.
    pendingResume.forEach(function(el) {
      try { if (el.paused) el.play().catch(function() {}); } catch(e) {}
    });
  }

  // Persistent touchstart listener — fires on every user touch while unmuted.
  // Retries AudioContext resume and HTML5 element .play() until all succeed.
  // Registered once at script init using capture phase so game code stopPropagation
  // cannot prevent it from running.
  //
  // Why persistent instead of { once: true }:
  // - postMessage delivery is async; the user may touch before unmuteAllAudio()
  //   processes the receive_audio_unmute message, consuming a { once: true } slot
  // - iOS may reject .play() on the first attempt but allow it on subsequent ones
  document.addEventListener('touchstart', function() {
    if (isMuted) return;
    // Retry AudioContexts on every touch (handles 'interrupted' / 'suspended')
    resumeAllContexts();
    // Retry HTML5 elements still waiting to resume
    if (pendingResume.length === 0) return;
    var toTry = pendingResume;
    pendingResume = [];
    toTry.forEach(function(el) {
      try {
        if (!el.paused) return; // Already playing — no action needed
        el.play().catch(function() {
          pendingResume.push(el); // Failed — retry on next touch
        });
      } catch(e) {
        pendingResume.push(el);
      }
    });
  }, { passive: true, capture: true });

  function collectMediaFromNode(node) {
    if (!node || node.nodeType !== 1) return [];
    var elements = [];
    if (node.tagName === 'AUDIO' || node.tagName === 'VIDEO') {
      elements.push(node);
    }
    if (typeof node.querySelectorAll === 'function') {
      node.querySelectorAll('audio, video').forEach(function(el) {
        elements.push(el);
      });
    }
    return elements;
  }

  document.addEventListener('play', function(event) {
    if (!isMuted) return;
    var target = event.target;
    if (!target || typeof target.pause !== 'function') return;
    lockElementAudio(target);
  }, true);

  document.addEventListener('playing', function(event) {
    if (!isMuted) return;
    var target = event.target;
    if (!target || typeof target.pause !== 'function') return;
    lockElementAudio(target);
  }, true);

  var mutationObserverTarget = document.documentElement || document;
  if (typeof MutationObserver !== 'undefined' &amp;&amp; mutationObserverTarget) {
    var mediaMutationObserver = new MutationObserver(function(mutations) {
      if (!isMuted) return;
      mutations.forEach(function(mutation) {
        mutation.addedNodes.forEach(function(node) {
          collectMediaFromNode(node).forEach(function(el) {
            lockElementAudio(el);
          });
        });
      });
    });
    mediaMutationObserver.observe(mutationObserverTarget, {
      childList: true,
      subtree: true,
    });
  }

  // ========================================================================
  // Audibility observer — parent-facing signal for feed perf dashboard.
  //
  // Fact measured: "is this iframe currently producing audible output?"
  //   - Based on real &lt;audio&gt;/&lt;video&gt; element state and AudioContext state
  //   - Not based on parent mute/unmute commands (those only state intent)
  //
  // Why this matters:
  //   - "should have played but didn't" — card visible, no start event
  //   - "played after it shouldn't" — audio duration &gt; card visible duration
  // Both failure modes are invisible from the parent side alone.
  //
  // Multi-cycle per card is supported naturally: every mute/unmute/tab
  // switch/user action that flips real audibility emits a new event pair.
  // ========================================================================
  var lastAudibleState = false;
  var audibilityCheckScheduled = false;

  function isElementAudible(el) {
    if (!el) return false;
    try {
      if (el.paused || el.ended || el.muted) return false;
      var vol = typeof el.volume === 'number' ? el.volume : 1;
      if (vol &lt;= 0) return false;
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  function computeAudible() {
    var elements = collectTrackedMediaElements();
    for (var i = 0; i &lt; elements.length; i++) {
      if (isElementAudible(elements[i])) return true;
    }
    for (var j = 0; j &lt; audioContexts.length; j++) {
      var ctx = audioContexts[j];
      if (ctx &amp;&amp; ctx.state === 'running') return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  function notifyAudibilityChange() {
    var audible = computeAudible();
    if (audible === lastAudibleState) return;
    lastAudibleState = audible;
    // Feed perf 采样门禁：父层已在注入时决定这轮是否上报。
    // 状态位 lastAudibleState 仍然更新，保证后续 started/stopped 仍能配对。
    if (window.__SEKAI_FEED_PERF_SAMPLED__ === false) return;
    try {
      window.parent.postMessage({
        type: audible
          ? 'sekai_iframe_audio_playing_started'
          : 'sekai_iframe_audio_playing_stopped',
        timestamp: Date.now(),
      }, '*');
    } catch (e) {
      // Ignore postMessage failures (parent window may be gone)
    }
  }

  // scheduleAudibilityCheck is also referenced from PatchedAudioContext's
  // statechange listener above — hoisted function declaration resolves OK.
  function scheduleAudibilityCheck() {
    if (audibilityCheckScheduled) return;
    audibilityCheckScheduled = true;
    // Microtask: batch multiple synchronous state changes (e.g. mute pauses
    // 3 elements + suspends 1 context in sequence) into a single notification.
    Promise.resolve().then(function() {
      audibilityCheckScheduled = false;
      notifyAudibilityChange();
    });
  }

  // Capture phase so game code calling stopPropagation cannot hide these.
  // Only react to events from &lt;audio&gt; / &lt;video&gt; elements.
  ['play', 'playing', 'pause', 'ended', 'volumechange', 'emptied'].forEach(function(evName) {
    document.addEventListener(evName, function(event) {
      var target = event.target;
      var tag = target &amp;&amp; target.tagName;
      if (tag !== 'AUDIO' &amp;&amp; tag !== 'VIDEO') return;
      scheduleAudibilityCheck();
    }, true);
  });

  window.addEventListener('message', function(event) {
    if (!event.data) return;

    if (event.data.type === 'receive_audio_mute' ||
        (event.data.origin === 'sekai_gaming_iframe_api' &amp;&amp; event.data.type === 'receive_audio_mute')) {
      muteAllAudio();
    }

    if (event.data.type === 'receive_audio_unmute' ||
        (event.data.origin === 'sekai_gaming_iframe_api' &amp;&amp; event.data.type === 'receive_audio_unmute')) {
      unmuteAllAudio();
    }
  });
})();
&lt;/script&gt;

&lt;script&gt;
(function() {
  var ORIGIN = 'sekai_gaming_iframe_api';

  // --- Shared helper: find &lt;audio&gt; elements by URL and set volume ---
  function applyVolumeByUrl(url, volume) {
    if (!url) return;
    var vol = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number(volume)));
    if (isNaN(vol)) return;
    document.querySelectorAll('audio').forEach(function(el) {
      var src = el.src || el.currentSrc || '';
      if (src === url) {
        el.volume = vol;
      }
    });
  }

  // --- Initial volume on game load ---
  // Reads window.sekaiEditable (written into the HTML by the backend) and applies
  // any volume values to matching &lt;audio&gt; elements. The LLM-generated game code
  // does not read the volume field, so we apply it here.
  function applyInitialVolumes() {
    var meta = window.sekaiEditable;
    if (!meta) return;
    var items = (meta.music || []).concat(meta.sfx || []);
    items.forEach(function(item) {
      if (item.value &amp;&amp; item.volume !== undefined &amp;&amp; item.volume !== null) {
        applyVolumeByUrl(item.value, item.volume);
      }
    });
  }

  // Run as soon as DOM is parsed, then retry after short delays to catch
  // audio elements created during game initialization (e.g. after DOMContentLoaded).
  if (document.readyState !== 'loading') {
    applyInitialVolumes();
  } else {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', applyInitialVolumes);
  }
  setTimeout(applyInitialVolumes, 500);
  setTimeout(applyInitialVolumes, 1500);

  // --- Real-time volume control (postMessage from editor) ---
  window.addEventListener('message', function(event) {
    var data = event.data;
    if (!data || data.origin !== ORIGIN) return;

    var payload = data.data;
    if (!payload) return;

    if (data.type === 'set_audio_volume') {
      // Real-time drag path: LLM code never sees this type
      applyVolumeByUrl(payload.url, payload.volume);
    } else if (data.type === 'apply_change' &amp;&amp; payload.volume !== undefined &amp;&amp; payload.volume !== null) {
      // Commit path (on release): runs before LLM handler via capture phase
      applyVolumeByUrl(payload.value, payload.volume);
    }
  }, true); // capture phase — runs before LLM game code
})();
&lt;/script&gt;

&lt;script&gt;
(function() {
  var ORIGIN = 'sekai_gaming_iframe_api';

  // Handle the font data URL response from the parent window.
  // The parent fetched the font bytes and encoded them as a base64 data URL
  // so we can inject @font-face without any cross-origin network request.
  window.addEventListener('message', function(event) {
    var msg = event.data;
    if (!msg || msg.type !== 'sekai_font_data_response') return;

    var prevStyle = document.getElementById('sekai-font-' + msg.itemId);
    if (prevStyle) prevStyle.parentNode.removeChild(prevStyle);

    var style = document.createElement('style');
    style.id = 'sekai-font-' + msg.itemId;
    // font-weight range covers all weights so we don't need per-weight rules.
    style.textContent = "@font-face { font-family: '" + msg.family + "'; src: url('" + msg.dataUrl + "'); font-weight: 100 900; }";
    document.head.appendChild(style);

    if (msg.cssVar) {
      document.documentElement.style.setProperty(msg.cssVar, "'" + msg.family + "'");
    }
  });

  window.addEventListener('message', function(event) {
    var data = event.data;
    if (!data || data.origin !== ORIGIN) return;
    if (data.type !== 'apply_change') return;

    var payload = data.data;
    if (!payload || !payload.value) return;

    var meta = window.sekaiEditable;
    if (!meta) return;

    // --- Font handling ---
    // No type guard — LLM-generated sekaiEditable often omits the type field.
    if (meta.fonts) {
      for (var i = 0; i &lt; meta.fonts.length; i++) {
        var font = meta.fonts[i];
        if (font.id === payload.id) {
          // Update sekaiEditable synchronously so get_current_html saves the new value.
          font.value = payload.value;
          if (payload.name) font.name = payload.name;
          // Ask the parent to fetch and return the font as a data URL.
          // Avoids WKWebView blob-iframe CORS restrictions on external font fetches.
          window.parent.postMessage({
            type: 'sekai_request_font_data',
            itemId: font.id,
            fontUrl: payload.value,
            family: font.fallback || ('sekai-swap-' + font.id),
            cssVar: font.cssVar || null,
          }, '*');
          return;
        }
      }
    }

    // --- Model handling ---
    if (meta.models) {
      for (var j = 0; j &lt; meta.models.length; j++) {
        var model = meta.models[j];
        if (model.id === payload.id) {
          // Update sekaiEditable so get_current_html saves the new value.
          model.value = payload.value;
          if (payload.name) model.name = payload.name;
          return;
        }
      }
    }
  }, true); // capture phase — runs before LLM apply_change handler
})();
&lt;/script&gt;

&lt;script&gt;
(function() {
  // Set styles to contain scroll and prevent propagation to parent
  // CRITICAL: touch-action:none is the PRIMARY scroll blocker - it's handled by the compositor
  // without waiting for JS, preventing WebView freezes during rapid swipes
  var style = document.createElement('style');
  style.textContent = 'html,body{overscroll-behavior:contain!important;height:100%!important;-webkit-user-select:none!important;user-select:none!important;touch-action:none!important;}';
  document.head.appendChild(style);

  var scrollableTarget = null;
  var touchStartY = 0;

  function findScrollableAncestor(el) {
    while (el &amp;&amp; el !== document.body &amp;&amp; el !== document.documentElement) {
      var cs = window.getComputedStyle(el);
      if ((cs.overflowY === 'auto' || cs.overflowY === 'scroll') &amp;&amp; el.scrollHeight &gt; el.clientHeight) {
        return el;
      }
      el = el.parentElement;
    }
    return null;
  }

  document.addEventListener('touchstart', function(e) {
    if (e.touches.length !== 1) {
      scrollableTarget = null;
      return;
    }

    touchStartY = e.touches[0].clientY;
    scrollableTarget = findScrollableAncestor(e.target);

    if (scrollableTarget) {
      scrollableTarget.style.touchAction = 'pan-y';
    }
  }, { passive: true, capture: true });

  var resetScrollState = function() {
    if (scrollableTarget) {
      scrollableTarget.style.touchAction = '';
      scrollableTarget = null;
    }
  };

  document.addEventListener('touchend', resetScrollState, { passive: true, capture: true });
  document.addEventListener('touchcancel', resetScrollState, { passive: true, capture: true });

  // iOS WKWebView fix: CSS touch-action:none inside an iframe does NOT
  // prevent the parent WebView scroll container from scrolling. A single
  // non-passive touchmove handler with preventDefault() is the only
  // reliable cross-iframe scroll blocker on iOS.
  // Uses the original addEventListener saved by TOUCH_LISTENER_PATCH_SCRIPT
  // to bypass the passive-force patch applied to game code listeners.
  var origAel = window.__sekai_orig_ael;
  if (origAel) {
    origAel.call(document, 'touchmove', function(e) {
      if (e.touches.length !== 1 || !e.cancelable) return;

      if (!scrollableTarget) {
        e.preventDefault();
        return;
      }

      var st = scrollableTarget;
      var atTop = st.scrollTop &lt;= 0;
      var atBottom = st.scrollTop &gt;= st.scrollHeight - st.clientHeight - 1;
      var currentY = e.touches[0].clientY;

      if ((atTop &amp;&amp; atBottom) ||
          (atTop &amp;&amp; currentY &gt; touchStartY) ||
          (atBottom &amp;&amp; currentY &lt; touchStartY)) {
        e.preventDefault();
      }
    }, { passive: false, capture: true });
  }

  // Notify parent when input is focused (for keyboard handling)
  document.addEventListener('focusin', function(e) {
    var t = e.target;
    if (t.tagName === 'INPUT' || t.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' || t.isContentEditable) {
      window.parent.postMessage({ type: 'iframe-focusin' }, '*');
    }
  }, true);

  document.addEventListener('focusout', function(e) {
    var t = e.target;
    if (t.tagName === 'INPUT' || t.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' || t.isContentEditable) {
      // Check if focus is moving to another focusable element (input switching)
      // If so, don't send focusout - the keyboard stays open
      var related = e.relatedTarget;
      if (related &amp;&amp; (related.tagName === 'INPUT' || related.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' || related.isContentEditable)) {
        return; // Skip focusout when switching between inputs
      }
      window.parent.postMessage({ type: 'iframe-focusout' }, '*');
    }
  }, true);
})();
&lt;/script&gt;&lt;style&gt;html,body{overscroll-behavior:contain!important;height:100%!important;-webkit-user-select:none!important;user-select:none!important;touch-action:none!important;}&lt;/style&gt;

&lt;script&gt;
(function() {
  var viewportStyle = document.createElement('style');
  viewportStyle.id = 'sekai-viewport-style';
  document.head.appendChild(viewportStyle);

  var currentHeight = null;

  function applyHeight(height) {
    if (height === currentHeight) return;
    currentHeight = height;

    var transition = 'transition: height 0.25s cubic-bezier(0.32, 0, 0.68, 1) !important;';

    if (typeof height === 'number' &amp;&amp; height &gt; 0) {
      viewportStyle.textContent =
        ':root { --sekai-viewport-height: ' + height + 'px; }' +
        'html { height: ' + height + 'px !important; overflow: hidden !important; ' + transition + ' }' +
        'body { height: ' + height + 'px !important; overflow: hidden !important; ' + transition + ' }';
    } else {
      viewportStyle.textContent =
        ':root { --sekai-viewport-height: 100%; }' +
        'html { height: 100% !important; overflow: hidden !important; }' +
        'body { height: 100% !important; overflow: hidden !important; }';
    }
  }

  window.addEventListener('message', function(event) {
    if (event.data &amp;&amp; event.data.type === 'viewport_height_change') {
      var height = typeof event.data.height === 'number' ? event.data.height : null;
      applyHeight(height);
    }
  });

  // Notify parent that viewport handler is ready (handshake)
  window.parent.postMessage({ type: 'viewport_ready' }, '*');
})();
&lt;/script&gt;&lt;style id="sekai-viewport-style"&gt;&lt;/style&gt;

&lt;script&gt;
(function() {
  var touchStartY = null;
  var lastSentTime = 0;
  var SWIPE_THRESHOLD = 100;
  var DEBOUNCE_MS = 500; // Prevent multiple events from single swipe

  document.addEventListener('touchstart', function(e) {
    if (e.touches.length === 1) {
      touchStartY = e.touches[0].clientY;
    }
  }, { passive: true, capture: true });

  document.addEventListener('touchend', function(e) {
    if (touchStartY !== null &amp;&amp; e.changedTouches.length === 1) {
      var now = Date.now();
      var deltaY = touchStartY - e.changedTouches[0].clientY;
      var timeSinceLast = now - lastSentTime;
      // Only send if above threshold AND not within debounce window
      if (deltaY &gt;= SWIPE_THRESHOLD &amp;&amp; timeSinceLast &gt;= DEBOUNCE_MS) {
        lastSentTime = now;
        window.parent.postMessage({
          type: 'sekai_iframe_swipe_up',
          deltaY: deltaY,
          timestamp: now
        }, '*');
      }
    }
    touchStartY = null;
  }, { passive: true, capture: true });

  document.addEventListener('touchcancel', function() {
    touchStartY = null;
  }, { passive: true, capture: true });
})();
&lt;/script&gt;

&lt;script&gt;
(function() {

  function urlToBase64(url) {
    return fetch(url)
      .then(function(res) { return res.blob(); })
      .then(function(blob) {
        return new Promise(function(resolve) {
          var reader = new FileReader();
          reader.onload = function() { resolve(reader.result); };
          reader.readAsDataURL(blob);
        });
      })
      .catch(function() { return null; });
  }

  function inlineImages(doc) {
    var imgs = Array.prototype.slice.call(doc.querySelectorAll('img[src]'));
    return Promise.all(imgs.map(function(img) {
      return urlToBase64(img.src).then(function(base64) {
        if (base64) img.setAttribute('src', base64);
      });
    }));
  }

  function inlineCssUrls(css, baseUrl) {
    var urlPattern = /url(s*([^)]+)s*)/g;
    var matches = [];
    var match;
    while ((match = urlPattern.exec(css)) !== null) {
      var url = match[1].trim().replace(/^['"]|['"]$/g, '');
      if (!url.startsWith('data:') &amp;&amp; !url.startsWith('#')) {
        matches.push({ full: match[0], url: url });
      }
    }
    if (matches.length === 0) return Promise.resolve(css);

    return Promise.all(matches.map(function(m) {
      var resolved;
      try { resolved = new URL(m.url, baseUrl).href; } catch(e) { return Promise.resolve(null); }
      return urlToBase64(resolved).then(function(base64) {
        return base64 ? { original: m.full, replacement: 'url(' + base64 + ')' } : null;
      });
    })).then(function(results) {
      results.forEach(function(r) {
        if (r) css = css.split(r.original).join(r.replacement);
      });
      return css;
    });
  }

  function inlineStylesheets(doc) {
    var links = Array.prototype.slice.call(doc.querySelectorAll('link[rel="stylesheet"]'));
    return Promise.all(links.map(function(link) {
      var href = link.href;
      return fetch(href)
        .then(function(res) { return res.text(); })
        .then(function(css) { return inlineCssUrls(css, href); })
        .then(function(css) {
          var style = doc.createElement('style');
          style.textContent = css;
          link.parentNode.replaceChild(style, link);
        })
        .catch(function() {});
    }));
  }

  function captureFullSnapshot() {
    var cloned = document.documentElement.cloneNode(true);
    var tempDoc = document.implementation.createHTMLDocument('');
    tempDoc.documentElement.replaceWith(cloned);

    // Remove all script tags so the snapshot is a frozen DOM state —
    // not a re-playable game. Without this, opening the file re-runs
    // all scripts and resets the game, destroying the captured state.
    var scripts = Array.prototype.slice.call(tempDoc.querySelectorAll('script'));
    scripts.forEach(function(s) { s.parentNode.removeChild(s); });

    return Promise.all([
      inlineImages(tempDoc),
      inlineStylesheets(tempDoc),
    ]).then(function() {
      return '&lt;!DOCTYPE html&gt;' + tempDoc.documentElement.outerHTML;
    });
  }

  var _runtimeErrors = [];

  window.addEventListener('error', function(e) {
    _runtimeErrors.push({
      type: 'error',
      message: e.message,
      source: e.filename,
      line: e.lineno,
      col: e.colno,
      stack: e.error ? e.error.stack : null,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    });
  });

  window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection', function(e) {
    _runtimeErrors.push({
      type: 'unhandledrejection',
      message: e.reason instanceof Error ? e.reason.message : String(e.reason),
      stack: e.reason instanceof Error ? e.reason.stack : null,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    });
  });

  // 监听来自父页面的指令
  window.addEventListener('message', function(e) {
    if (!e.data || e.data.type !== 'TAKE_SNAPSHOT') return;

    captureFullSnapshot().then(function(html) {
      window.parent.postMessage({
        type: 'SNAPSHOT_RESULT',
        payload: {
          html: html,
          errors: _runtimeErrors.slice(),
        }
      }, '*');
    });
  });

})();
&lt;/script&gt;

      &lt;!-- BGM Audio element bound to Sekai slot --&gt;
      &lt;audio id="bgm" data-sekai-slot="music:lofi-studio-ambient" loop="" src="https://prod-data.sekai.chat/aiu-bgm/b8a8a614-2f25-4099-9fde-ed3800377ef1_Places (Lo-Fi Version)_normalized.mp3"&gt;&lt;/audio&gt;

    &lt;!-- Application Script --&gt;
    &lt;script type="module"&gt;
      import { applyAllEditableValues, getConfig, initBGM, vibrate } from './js/sekai/bridge/index.js';

      //#region @sekai:state · State and canvas engine
      const CANVAS_WIDTH = 800;
      const CANVAS_HEIGHT = 800;

      const state = {
        tool: 'draw',            // 'draw' | 'warp' | 'cut' | 'presets' | 'shape' | 'text' | 'brush-custom'
        brushType: 'solid',
        brushColor: '#3B82F6',
        brushSize: 16,
        brushMode: 'paint',      // 'paint' | 'erase'
        warpMode: 'liquify',     // 'liquify' | 'corner'
        warpRadius: 70,
        meshPoints: [
          { x: 0, y: 0 },
          { x: CANVAS_WIDTH, y: 0 },
          { x: CANVAS_WIDTH, y: CANVAS_HEIGHT },
          { x: 0, y: CANVAS_HEIGHT }
        ],
        activeCornerIndex: -1,
        lassoPoints: [],
        isDrawing: false,
        lastX: 0,
        lastY: 0,
        history: [],
        historyIndex: -1,
        hasImage: false,
        activeCategory: 'all',
        searchQuery: '',
      };

      let mainCanvas, mainCtx;
      let overlayCanvas, overlayCtx;
      let containerEl;
      let originalImageData = null;

      // Web Audio synthesizer
      let audioCtx = null;
      function getAudioContext() {
        if (!audioCtx) {
          const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
          if (AudioContext) audioCtx = new AudioContext();
        }
        if (audioCtx &amp;&amp; audioCtx.state === 'suspended') {
          audioCtx.resume().catch(() =&gt; {});
        }
        return audioCtx;
      }

      function playSound(type) {
        try {
          const ctx = getAudioContext();
          if (!ctx) return;
          const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
          const gain = ctx.createGain();
          osc.connect(gain);
          gain.connect(ctx.destination);
          const t = ctx.currentTime;

          if (type === 'click') {
            osc.type = 'sine';
            osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(440, t);
            osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(880, t + 0.05);
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.12, t);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + 0.05);
            osc.start(t);
            osc.stop(t + 0.05);
          } else if (type === 'brush') {
            osc.type = 'triangle';
            osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(220, t);
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.03, t);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + 0.04);
            osc.start(t);
            osc.stop(t + 0.04);
          } else if (type === 'warp') {
            osc.type = 'sine';
            osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(320, t);
            osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(240, t + 0.08);
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.1, t);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + 0.08);
            osc.start(t);
            osc.stop(t + 0.08);
          } else if (type === 'snip') {
            osc.type = 'square';
            osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(600, t);
            osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(300, t + 0.1);
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.1, t);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + 0.1);
            osc.start(t);
            osc.stop(t + 0.1);
          } else if (type === 'magic') {
            osc.type = 'sine';
            osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(520, t);
            osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1040, t + 0.12);
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.12, t);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + 0.12);
            osc.start(t);
            osc.stop(t + 0.12);
          }
        } catch (e) {}
      }

      //#endregion @sekai:state

      //#region @sekai:canvas · Canvas Setup &amp; History
      function initCanvas() {
        mainCanvas = document.getElementById('main-canvas');
        mainCtx = mainCanvas.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true });

        overlayCanvas = document.getElementById('overlay-canvas');
        overlayCtx = overlayCanvas.getContext('2d');

        containerEl = document.getElementById('canvas-container');

        mainCanvas.width = CANVAS_WIDTH;
        mainCanvas.height = CANVAS_HEIGHT;
        overlayCanvas.width = CANVAS_WIDTH;
        overlayCanvas.height = CANVAS_HEIGHT;

        resizeViewport();
        window.addEventListener('resize', resizeViewport);

        // Load initial preset art
        loadSampleArt('portrait');
      }

      function resizeViewport() {
        const workspace = document.getElementById('workspace');
        if (!workspace) return;
        const rect = workspace.getBoundingClientRect();
        const pad = 20;
        const availW = Math.max(rect.width - pad, 180);
        const availH = Math.max(rect.height - pad, 180);

        const aspect = CANVAS_WIDTH / CANVAS_HEIGHT;
        let displayW = availW;
        let displayH = displayW / aspect;

        if (displayH &gt; availH) {
          displayH = availH;
          displayW = displayH * aspect;
        }

        containerEl.style.width = `${Math.round(displayW)}px`;
        containerEl.style.height = `${Math.round(displayH)}px`;
      }

      function saveHistorySnapshot() {
        try {
          const imgData = mainCtx.getImageData(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
          state.history = state.history.slice(0, state.historyIndex + 1);
          state.history.push(imgData);
          if (state.history.length &gt; 20) {
            state.history.shift();
          }
          state.historyIndex = state.history.length - 1;
          updateUndoRedoUI();
        } catch (e) {
          console.warn('History snapshot failed', e);
        }
      }

      function undo() {
        if (state.historyIndex &gt; 0) {
          state.historyIndex--;
          const snapshot = state.history[state.historyIndex];
          mainCtx.putImageData(snapshot, 0, 0);
          updateUndoRedoUI();
          showToast('Undone');
          vibrate(40);
          playSound('click');
        }
      }

      function redo() {
        if (state.historyIndex &lt; state.history.length - 1) {
          state.historyIndex++;
          const snapshot = state.history[state.historyIndex];
          mainCtx.putImageData(snapshot, 0, 0);
          updateUndoRedoUI();
          showToast('Redone');
          vibrate(40);
          playSound('click');
        }
      }

      function updateUndoRedoUI() {
        const btnUndo = document.getElementById('btn-undo');
        const btnRedo = document.getElementById('btn-redo');
        if (btnUndo) btnUndo.disabled = state.historyIndex &lt;= 0;
        if (btnRedo) btnRedo.disabled = state.historyIndex &gt;= state.history.length - 1;
      }

      //#endregion @sekai:canvas

      //#region @sekai:art · Image Loading and Procedural Presets
      function drawFallbackPortrait(ctx) {
        const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
        grad.addColorStop(0, '#1e1b4b');
        grad.addColorStop(0.5, '#312e81');
        grad.addColorStop(1, '#0f172a');
        ctx.fillStyle = grad;
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);

        const rad = ctx.createRadialGradient(400, 380, 50, 400, 380, 350);
        rad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.4)');
        rad.addColorStop(0.5, 'rgba(139, 92, 246, 0.25)');
        rad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)');
        ctx.fillStyle = rad;
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);

        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(CANVAS_WIDTH / 2, CANVAS_HEIGHT / 2);

        // Body / Shoulders
        ctx.fillStyle = '#1e293b';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(0, 260, 220, 160, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        // Jacket collar
        ctx.fillStyle = '#334155';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(-100, 160);
        ctx.lineTo(0, 280);
        ctx.lineTo(100, 160);
        ctx.lineTo(130, 280);
        ctx.lineTo(-130, 280);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();

        // Head
        ctx.fillStyle = '#f87171';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(0, 40, 110, 140, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        // Hair / Neon helmet
        ctx.fillStyle = '#38bdf8';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(0, 0, 125, Math.PI, 0, false);
        ctx.lineTo(125, 40);
        ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, -20, -125, 40);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();

        // Cyber visor glasses
        ctx.fillStyle = '#facc15';
        ctx.shadowColor = '#facc15';
        ctx.shadowBlur = 20;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.roundRect(-80, 20, 160, 38, 8);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.shadowBlur = 0;

        // Visor reflection
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.7)';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(-60, 26);
        ctx.lineTo(-20, 26);
        ctx.lineTo(-40, 52);
        ctx.lineTo(-70, 52);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();

        ctx.restore();

        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.2)';
        ctx.font = 'bold 20px system-ui, sans-serif';
        ctx.textAlign = 'right';
        ctx.fillText('MINIEDIT // 260 TOOLS READY', CANVAS_WIDTH - 30, CANVAS_HEIGHT - 30);
      }

      function drawFallbackLandscape(ctx) {
        const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
        grad.addColorStop(0, '#030712');
        grad.addColorStop(0.45, '#4c0519');
        grad.addColorStop(0.55, '#fb7185');
        grad.addColorStop(0.56, '#0f172a');
        grad.addColorStop(1, '#020617');
        ctx.fillStyle = grad;
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);

        // Giant Retro Sun
        const sunGrad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 180, 0, 440);
        sunGrad.addColorStop(0, '#fde047');
        sunGrad.addColorStop(1, '#f43f5e');
        ctx.fillStyle = sunGrad;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(400, 380, 160, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        // Sun scanlines
        ctx.fillStyle = '#030712';
        for (let y = 340; y &lt; 440; y += 14) {
          ctx.fillRect(200, y, 400, 4 + (y - 340) * 0.08);
        }

        // Mountains
        ctx.fillStyle = '#1e1b4b';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(0, 440);
        ctx.lineTo(160, 310);
        ctx.lineTo(280, 440);
        ctx.lineTo(480, 290);
        ctx.lineTo(650, 440);
        ctx.lineTo(800, 330);
        ctx.lineTo(800, 440);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();

        // Perspective Grid
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#06b6d4';
        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
        const horizon = 440;
        for (let i = 1; i &lt;= 10; i++) {
          const y = horizon + Math.pow(i / 10, 2) * (CANVAS_HEIGHT - horizon);
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.moveTo(0, y);
          ctx.lineTo(CANVAS_WIDTH, y);
          ctx.stroke();
        }
        for (let x = -200; x &lt;= CANVAS_WIDTH + 200; x += 60) {
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.moveTo(400, horizon);
          ctx.lineTo(x, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
          ctx.stroke();
        }
      }

      function loadSampleArt(presetName) {
        mainCtx.clearRect(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
        if (presetName === 'portrait') {
          const sampleUrl = getConfig('images', 'sample-portrait', '');
          if (sampleUrl) {
            const img = new Image();
            img.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
            img.onload = () =&gt; {
              drawImageFitted(img);
              saveHistorySnapshot();
            };
            img.onerror = () =&gt; {
              drawFallbackPortrait(mainCtx);
              saveHistorySnapshot();
            };
            img.src = sampleUrl;
          } else {
            drawFallbackPortrait(mainCtx);
            saveHistorySnapshot();
          }
        } else if (presetName === 'landscape') {
          drawFallbackLandscape(mainCtx);
          saveHistorySnapshot();
        } else {
          mainCtx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
          mainCtx.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
          saveHistorySnapshot();
        }
        state.hasImage = true;
        resetMeshPoints();
      }

      function drawImageFitted(img) {
        mainCtx.fillStyle = '#121214';
        mainCtx.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);

        const imgAspect = img.width / img.height;
        const canvasAspect = CANVAS_WIDTH / CANVAS_HEIGHT;
        let dw, dh, dx, dy;

        if (imgAspect &gt; canvasAspect) {
          dw = CANVAS_WIDTH;
          dh = CANVAS_WIDTH / imgAspect;
          dx = 0;
          dy = (CANVAS_HEIGHT - dh) / 2;
        } else {
          dh = CANVAS_HEIGHT;
          dw = CANVAS_HEIGHT * imgAspect;
          dx = (CANVAS_WIDTH - dw) / 2;
          dy = 0;
        }

        mainCtx.drawImage(img, dx, dy, dw, dh);
      }

      function handleFileUpload(file) {
        if (!file || !file.type.startsWith('image/')) return;
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = (e) =&gt; {
          const img = new Image();
          img.onload = () =&gt; {
            drawImageFitted(img);
            saveHistorySnapshot();
            resetMeshPoints();
            showToast('Image Loaded');
            vibrate(50);
            playSound('click');
          };
          img.src = e.target.result;
        };
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
      }

      //#endregion @sekai:art

      //#region @sekai:tools · Drawing, Liquify Warp, Mesh Warp &amp; Lasso Cut

      function getCanvasCoordinates(e) {
        const rect = mainCanvas.getBoundingClientRect();
        const scaleX = CANVAS_WIDTH / rect.width;
        const scaleY = CANVAS_HEIGHT / rect.height;
        let clientX = e.clientX;
        let clientY = e.clientY;

        if (e.touches &amp;&amp; e.touches.length &gt; 0) {
          clientX = e.touches[0].clientX;
          clientY = e.touches[0].clientY;
        }

        return {
          x: Math.max(0, Math.min(CANVAS_WIDTH, (clientX - rect.left) * scaleX)),
          y: Math.max(0, Math.min(CANVAS_HEIGHT, (clientY - rect.top) * scaleY))
        };
      }

      // 1. Draw Brush Tool with custom brush styles
      function startBrush(pt) {
        state.isDrawing = true;
        state.lastX = pt.x;
        state.lastY = pt.y;

        mainCtx.save();
        mainCtx.lineCap = 'round';
        mainCtx.lineJoin = 'round';
        mainCtx.lineWidth = state.brushSize;

        if (state.brushMode === 'erase') {
          mainCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out';
          mainCtx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,1)';
          mainCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,1)';
        } else {
          mainCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
          mainCtx.strokeStyle = state.brushColor;
          mainCtx.fillStyle = state.brushColor;
        }

        if (state.brushType === 'neon') {
          mainCtx.shadowColor = state.brushColor;
          mainCtx.shadowBlur = 15;
        } else if (state.brushType === 'stars') {
          drawStarStamp(mainCtx, pt.x, pt.y, state.brushSize * 0.8, state.brushColor);
        } else if (state.brushType === 'hearts') {
          drawHeartStamp(mainCtx, pt.x, pt.y, state.brushSize * 0.8, state.brushColor);
        }

        // Draw dot
        mainCtx.beginPath();
        mainCtx.arc(pt.x, pt.y, state.brushSize / 2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        mainCtx.fill();
        mainCtx.restore();

        drawBrushCursor(pt);
        playSound('brush');
      }

      function moveBrush(pt) {
        if (!state.isDrawing) {
          drawBrushCursor(pt);
          return;
        }

        mainCtx.save();
        mainCtx.lineCap = 'round';
        mainCtx.lineJoin = 'round';
        mainCtx.lineWidth = state.brushSize;

        if (state.brushMode === 'erase') {
          mainCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out';
          mainCtx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,1)';
        } else {
          mainCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
          mainCtx.strokeStyle = state.brushColor;
        }

        if (state.brushType === 'neon') {
          mainCtx.shadowColor = state.brushColor;
          mainCtx.shadowBlur = 15;
        } else if (state.brushType === 'spray') {
          for (let i = 0; i &lt; 15; i++) {
            const rad = Math.random() * state.brushSize;
            const ang = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
            mainCtx.fillStyle = state.brushColor;
            mainCtx.fillRect(pt.x + Math.cos(ang) * rad, pt.y + Math.sin(ang) * rad, 2, 2);
          }
        } else if (state.brushType === 'rainbow') {
          const hue = (Date.now() / 8) % 360;
          mainCtx.strokeStyle = `hsl(${hue}, 100%, 55%)`;
        }

        if (state.brushType !== 'spray') {
          mainCtx.beginPath();
          mainCtx.moveTo(state.lastX, state.lastY);
          mainCtx.lineTo(pt.x, pt.y);
          mainCtx.stroke();
        }

        mainCtx.restore();

        state.lastX = pt.x;
        state.lastY = pt.y;
        drawBrushCursor(pt);
      }

      function endBrush() {
        if (state.isDrawing) {
          state.isDrawing = false;
          saveHistorySnapshot();
          clearOverlay();
        }
      }

      function drawBrushCursor(pt) {
        clearOverlay();
        overlayCtx.save();
        overlayCtx.strokeStyle = state.brushMode === 'erase' ? '#ef4444' : '#38bdf8';
        overlayCtx.lineWidth = 1.5;
        overlayCtx.setLineDash([4, 4]);
        overlayCtx.beginPath();
        overlayCtx.arc(pt.x, pt.y, state.brushSize / 2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        overlayCtx.stroke();
        overlayCtx.restore();
      }

      function drawStarStamp(ctx, cx, cy, r, color) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.fillStyle = color;
        ctx.beginPath();
        for (let i = 0; i &lt; 5; i++) {
          ctx.lineTo(Math.cos((18 + i * 72) * 0.01745) * r + cx, -Math.sin((18 + i * 72) * 0.01745) * r + cy);
          ctx.lineTo(Math.cos((54 + i * 72) * 0.01745) * (r * 0.45) + cx, -Math.sin((54 + i * 72) * 0.01745) * (r * 0.45) + cy);
        }
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.restore();
      }

      function drawHeartStamp(ctx, cx, cy, size, color) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.fillStyle = color;
        ctx.beginPath();
        const topCurveHeight = size * 0.3;
        ctx.moveTo(cx, cy + size * 0.3);
        ctx.bezierCurveTo(cx, cy - topCurveHeight, cx - size / 2, cy - topCurveHeight, cx - size / 2, cy + size * 0.1);
        ctx.bezierCurveTo(cx - size / 2, cy + size * 0.4, cx, cy + size * 0.65, cx, cy + size * 0.8);
        ctx.bezierCurveTo(cx, cy + size * 0.65, cx + size / 2, cy + size * 0.4, cx + size / 2, cy + size * 0.1);
        ctx.bezierCurveTo(cx + size / 2, cy - topCurveHeight, cx, cy - topCurveHeight, cx, cy + size * 0.3);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.restore();
      }

      // 2. Liquify Clay Push Warp
      function startLiquify(pt) {
        state.isDrawing = true;
        state.lastX = pt.x;
        state.lastY = pt.y;
        drawLiquifyCursor(pt);
        vibrate(30);
      }

      function moveLiquify(pt) {
        if (!state.isDrawing) {
          drawLiquifyCursor(pt);
          return;
        }

        const dx = pt.x - state.lastX;
        const dy = pt.y - state.lastY;
        const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);

        if (dist &gt; 1.5) {
          applyLiquifyPush(state.lastX, state.lastY, dx, dy, state.warpRadius);
          state.lastX = pt.x;
          state.lastY = pt.y;
          playSound('warp');
        }

        drawLiquifyCursor(pt);
      }

      function endLiquify() {
        if (state.isDrawing) {
          state.isDrawing = false;
          saveHistorySnapshot();
          clearOverlay();
        }
      }

      function applyLiquifyPush(cx, cy, moveX, moveY, radius) {
        const r = Math.round(radius);
        const minX = Math.max(0, Math.floor(cx - r));
        const minY = Math.max(0, Math.floor(cy - r));
        const maxX = Math.min(CANVAS_WIDTH, Math.ceil(cx + r));
        const maxY = Math.min(CANVAS_HEIGHT, Math.ceil(cy + r));
        const w = maxX - minX;
        const h = maxY - minY;

        if (w &lt;= 0 || h &lt;= 0) return;

        const srcData = mainCtx.getImageData(minX, minY, w, h);
        const src = srcData.data;
        const dstData = mainCtx.createImageData(w, h);
        const dst = dstData.data;

        dst.set(src);

        const rSq = r * r;
        const strength = 0.85;

        for (let py = 0; py &lt; h; py++) {
          const worldY = minY + py;
          const dy = worldY - cy;
          const dySq = dy * dy;

          for (let px = 0; px &lt; w; px++) {
            const worldX = minX + px;
            const dx = worldX - cx;
            const distSq = dx * dx + dySq;

            if (distSq &lt; rSq) {
              const factor = Math.pow(1 - distSq / rSq, 2) * strength;
              const sampleX = px - moveX * factor;
              const sampleY = py - moveY * factor;

              const x0 = Math.floor(sampleX);
              const y0 = Math.floor(sampleY);
              const x1 = Math.min(w - 1, x0 + 1);
              const y1 = Math.min(h - 1, y0 + 1);
              const fx = sampleX - x0;
              const fy = sampleY - y0;

              if (x0 &gt;= 0 &amp;&amp; x1 &lt; w &amp;&amp; y0 &gt;= 0 &amp;&amp; y1 &lt; h) {
                const dstIdx = (py * w + px) * 4;
                const idx00 = (y0 * w + x0) * 4;
                const idx10 = (y0 * w + x1) * 4;
                const idx01 = (y1 * w + x0) * 4;
                const idx11 = (y1 * w + x1) * 4;

                for (let c = 0; c &lt; 4; c++) {
                  const top = src[idx00 + c] * (1 - fx) + src[idx10 + c] * fx;
                  const bot = src[idx01 + c] * (1 - fx) + src[idx11 + c] * fx;
                  dst[dstIdx + c] = Math.round(top * (1 - fy) + bot * fy);
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }

        mainCtx.putImageData(dstData, minX, minY);
      }

      function drawLiquifyCursor(pt) {
        clearOverlay();
        overlayCtx.save();
        overlayCtx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.8)';
        overlayCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.08)';
        overlayCtx.lineWidth = 2;
        overlayCtx.beginPath();
        overlayCtx.arc(pt.x, pt.y, state.warpRadius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        overlayCtx.fill();
        overlayCtx.stroke();

        overlayCtx.strokeStyle = '#60a5fa';
        overlayCtx.lineWidth = 1.5;
        overlayCtx.beginPath();
        overlayCtx.moveTo(pt.x - 8, pt.y);
        overlayCtx.lineTo(pt.x + 8, pt.y);
        overlayCtx.moveTo(pt.x, pt.y - 8);
        overlayCtx.lineTo(pt.x, pt.y + 8);
        overlayCtx.stroke();
        overlayCtx.restore();
      }

      // 3. Corner Mesh Warp
      function resetMeshPoints() {
        state.meshPoints = [
          { x: 40, y: 40 },
          { x: CANVAS_WIDTH - 40, y: 40 },
          { x: CANVAS_WIDTH - 40, y: CANVAS_HEIGHT - 40 },
          { x: 40, y: CANVAS_HEIGHT - 40 }
        ];
        originalImageData = mainCtx.getImageData(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
        drawMeshOverlay();
      }

      function drawMeshOverlay() {
        clearOverlay();
        const pts = state.meshPoints;

        overlayCtx.save();
        overlayCtx.strokeStyle = '#3b82f6';
        overlayCtx.lineWidth = 2.5;
        overlayCtx.beginPath();
        overlayCtx.moveTo(pts[0].x, pts[0].y);
        overlayCtx.lineTo(pts[1].x, pts[1].y);
        overlayCtx.lineTo(pts[2].x, pts[2].y);
        overlayCtx.lineTo(pts[3].x, pts[3].y);
        overlayCtx.closePath();
        overlayCtx.stroke();

        overlayCtx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.35)';
        overlayCtx.lineWidth = 1;
        overlayCtx.setLineDash([6, 6]);
        overlayCtx.beginPath();
        overlayCtx.moveTo(pts[0].x, pts[0].y);
        overlayCtx.lineTo(pts[2].x, pts[2].y);
        overlayCtx.moveTo(pts[1].x, pts[1].y);
        overlayCtx.lineTo(pts[3].x, pts[3].y);
        overlayCtx.stroke();
        overlayCtx.setLineDash([]);

        pts.forEach((p, idx) =&gt; {
          overlayCtx.fillStyle = idx === state.activeCornerIndex ? '#10b981' : '#ffffff';
          overlayCtx.strokeStyle = '#1d4ed8';
          overlayCtx.lineWidth = 3;
          overlayCtx.beginPath();
          overlayCtx.arc(p.x, p.y, 12, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          overlayCtx.fill();
          overlayCtx.stroke();
        });
        overlayCtx.restore();
      }

      function startMeshDrag(pt) {
        state.activeCornerIndex = -1;
        const pts = state.meshPoints;
        for (let i = 0; i &lt; pts.length; i++) {
          if (Math.hypot(pt.x - pts[i].x, pt.y - pts[i].y) &lt; 36) {
            state.activeCornerIndex = i;
            vibrate(40);
            playSound('click');
            break;
          }
        }
        drawMeshOverlay();
      }

      function moveMeshDrag(pt) {
        if (state.activeCornerIndex !== -1) {
          state.meshPoints[state.activeCornerIndex].x = pt.x;
          state.meshPoints[state.activeCornerIndex].y = pt.y;
          drawMeshOverlay();
          renderMeshWarpLive();
        }
      }

      function endMeshDrag() {
        if (state.activeCornerIndex !== -1) {
          state.activeCornerIndex = -1;
          drawMeshOverlay();
        }
      }

      function renderMeshWarpLive() {
        if (!originalImageData) {
          originalImageData = mainCtx.getImageData(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
        }

        const tempCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        tempCanvas.width = CANVAS_WIDTH;
        tempCanvas.height = CANVAS_HEIGHT;
        const tempCtx = tempCanvas.getContext('2d');
        tempCtx.putImageData(originalImageData, 0, 0);

        mainCtx.fillStyle = '#121214';
        mainCtx.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);

        const gridX = 8;
        const gridY = 8;
        const p = state.meshPoints;

        function getMap(u, v) {
          const topX = p[0].x * (1 - u) + p[1].x * u;
          const topY = p[0].y * (1 - u) + p[1].y * u;
          const botX = p[3].x * (1 - u) + p[2].x * u;
          const botY = p[3].y * (1 - u) + p[2].y * u;
          return {
            x: topX * (1 - v) + botX * v,
            y: topY * (1 - v) + botY * v
          };
        }

        for (let y = 0; y &lt; gridY; y++) {
          for (let x = 0; x &lt; gridX; x++) {
            const u0 = x / gridX;
            const v0 = y / gridY;
            const u1 = (x + 1) / gridX;
            const v1 = (y + 1) / gridY;

            const p00 = getMap(u0, v0);
            const p10 = getMap(u1, v0);
            const p01 = getMap(u0, v1);
            const p11 = getMap(u1, v1);

            const sx0 = u0 * CANVAS_WIDTH;
            const sy0 = v0 * CANVAS_HEIGHT;
            const sw = CANVAS_WIDTH / gridX;
            const sh = CANVAS_HEIGHT / gridY;

            drawTexturedTriangle(mainCtx, tempCanvas, p00, p10, p01, sx0, sy0, sx0 + sw, sy0, sx0, sy0 + sh);
            drawTexturedTriangle(mainCtx, tempCanvas, p10, p11, p01, sx0 + sw, sy0, sx0 + sw, sy0 + sh, sx0, sy0 + sh);
          }
        }
      }

      function drawTexturedTriangle(ctx, img, p0, p1, p2, u0, v0, u1, v1, u2, v2) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(p0.x, p0.y);
        ctx.lineTo(p1.x, p1.y);
        ctx.lineTo(p2.x, p2.y);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.clip();

        const delta = (u0 - u2) * (v1 - v2) - (u1 - u2) * (v0 - v2);
        if (delta === 0) {
          ctx.restore();
          return;
        }

        const deltaA = (p0.x - p2.x) * (v1 - v2) - (p1.x - p2.x) * (v0 - v2);
        const deltaB = (u0 - u2) * (p1.x - p2.x) - (u1 - u2) * (p0.x - p2.x);
        const deltaC = p0.x * (u1 * v2 - u2 * v1) + p1.x * (u2 * v0 - u0 * v2) + p2.x * (u0 * v1 - u1 * v0);

        const deltaD = (p0.y - p2.y) * (v1 - v2) - (p1.y - p2.y) * (v0 - v2);
        const deltaE = (u0 - u2) * (p1.y - p2.y) - (u1 - u2) * (p0.y - p2.y);
        const deltaF = p0.y * (u1 * v2 - u2 * v1) + p1.y * (u2 * v0 - u0 * v2) + p2.y * (u0 * v1 - u1 * v0);

        ctx.transform(
          deltaA / delta, deltaD / delta,
          deltaB / delta, deltaE / delta,
          deltaC / delta, deltaF / delta
        );

        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
        ctx.restore();
      }

      function applyMeshWarp() {
        renderMeshWarpLive();
        originalImageData = mainCtx.getImageData(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
        saveHistorySnapshot();
        resetMeshPoints();
        showToast('Mesh Warp Applied');
        vibrate(50);
        playSound('snip');
      }

      // 4. Freehand Lasso Cut Tool
      function startLasso(pt) {
        state.isDrawing = true;
        state.lassoPoints = [pt];
        drawLassoOverlay();
        vibrate(30);
      }

      function moveLasso(pt) {
        if (state.isDrawing) {
          state.lassoPoints.push(pt);
          drawLassoOverlay();
        }
      }

      function endLasso() {
        if (state.isDrawing) {
          state.isDrawing = false;
          if (state.lassoPoints.length &gt; 3) {
            drawLassoOverlay(true);
            showToast('Lasso Shape Closed — Select Cut Action');
            vibrate(40);
          } else {
            clearLasso();
          }
        }
      }

      function drawLassoOverlay(closed = false) {
        clearOverlay();
        const pts = state.lassoPoints;
        if (pts.length &lt; 2) return;

        overlayCtx.save();
        overlayCtx.strokeStyle = '#ef4444';
        overlayCtx.lineWidth = 2;
        overlayCtx.setLineDash([5, 5]);

        overlayCtx.beginPath();
        overlayCtx.moveTo(pts[0].x, pts[0].y);
        for (let i = 1; i &lt; pts.length; i++) {
          overlayCtx.lineTo(pts[i].x, pts[i].y);
        }
        if (closed) {
          overlayCtx.closePath();
          overlayCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.18)';
          overlayCtx.fill();
        }
        overlayCtx.stroke();
        overlayCtx.restore();
      }

      function executeLassoCut(invert = false) {
        const pts = state.lassoPoints;
        if (pts.length &lt; 3) {
          showToast('Draw a lasso selection first');
          return;
        }

        mainCtx.save();
        if (invert) {
          const tempCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
          tempCanvas.width = CANVAS_WIDTH;
          tempCanvas.height = CANVAS_HEIGHT;
          const tempCtx = tempCanvas.getContext('2d');

          tempCtx.beginPath();
          tempCtx.moveTo(pts[0].x, pts[0].y);
          for (let i = 1; i &lt; pts.length; i++) tempCtx.lineTo(pts[i].x, pts[i].y);
          tempCtx.closePath();
          tempCtx.clip();
          tempCtx.drawImage(mainCanvas, 0, 0);

          mainCtx.clearRect(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
          mainCtx.drawImage(tempCanvas, 0, 0);
        } else {
          mainCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out';
          mainCtx.beginPath();
          mainCtx.moveTo(pts[0].x, pts[0].y);
          for (let i = 1; i &lt; pts.length; i++) mainCtx.lineTo(pts[i].x, pts[i].y);
          mainCtx.closePath();
          mainCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,1)';
          mainCtx.fill();
        }
        mainCtx.restore();

        saveHistorySnapshot();
        clearLasso();
        showToast(invert ? 'Cut (Invert) Applied' : 'Cut Out Applied');
        vibrate(60);
        playSound('snip');
      }

      function clearLasso() {
        state.lassoPoints = [];
        clearOverlay();
      }

      function clearOverlay() {
        overlayCtx.clearRect(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
      }

      //#endregion @sekai:tools

      //#region @sekai:hub · 260+ Useful Tools Registry &amp; Execution Engine

      // Core pixel manipulation helpers
      function applyPixelFilter(filterFn) {
        const imgData = mainCtx.getImageData(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
        const data = imgData.data;
        const len = data.length;
        for (let i = 0; i &lt; len; i += 4) {
          filterFn(data, i);
        }
        mainCtx.putImageData(imgData, 0, 0);
        saveHistorySnapshot();
      }

      function applyConvolutionMatrix(weights, bias = 0) {
        const side = Math.round(Math.sqrt(weights.length));
        const halfSide = Math.floor(side / 2);
        const srcData = mainCtx.getImageData(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
        const src = srcData.data;
        const dstData = mainCtx.createImageData(CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
        const dst = dstData.data;
        const w = CANVAS_WIDTH;
        const h = CANVAS_HEIGHT;

        for (let y = 0; y &lt; h; y++) {
          for (let x = 0; x &lt; w; x++) {
            const dstOff = (y * w + x) * 4;
            let r = 0, g = 0, b = 0;
            for (let cy = 0; cy &lt; side; cy++) {
              for (let cx = 0; cx &lt; side; cx++) {
                const scy = Math.min(h - 1, Math.max(0, y + cy - halfSide));
                const scx = Math.min(w - 1, Math.max(0, x + cx - halfSide));
                const srcOff = (scy * w + scx) * 4;
                const wt = weights[cy * side + cx];
                r += src[srcOff] * wt;
                g += src[srcOff + 1] * wt;
                b += src[srcOff + 2] * wt;
              }
            }
            dst[dstOff] = Math.min(255, Math.max(0, r + bias));
            dst[dstOff + 1] = Math.min(255, Math.max(0, g + bias));
            dst[dstOff + 2] = Math.min(255, Math.max(0, b + bias));
            dst[dstOff + 3] = src[dstOff + 3];
          }
        }
        mainCtx.putImageData(dstData, 0, 0);
        saveHistorySnapshot();
      }

      // Generate the comprehensive 260 Tools Catalog
      const TOOLS_CATALOG = [
        // 1. ADJUSTMENTS (~60)
        { id: 'adj-brightness-up', name: 'Brightness +20%', cat: 'adjust', desc: 'Lighten overall exposure', action: () =&gt; applyPixelFilter((d, i) =&gt; { d[i] = Math.min(255, d[i] + 35); d[i+1] = Math.min(255, d[i+1] + 35); d[i+2] = Math.min(255, d[i+2] + 35); }) },
        { id: 'adj-brightness-down', name: 'Brightness -20%', cat: 'adjust', desc: 'Darken overall exposure', action: () =&gt; applyPixelFilter((d, i) =&gt; { d[i] = Math.max(0, d[i] - 35); d[i+1] = Math.max(0, d[i+1] - 35); d[i+2] = Math.max(0, d[i+2] - 35); }) },
        { id: 'adj-contrast-high', name: 'High Contrast', cat: 'adjust', desc: 'Punch up shadows &amp; highlights', action: () =&gt; applyPixelFilter((d, i) =&gt; { for(let c=0;c&lt;3;c++) d[i+c] = Math.min(255, Math.max(0, 128 + 1.4 * (d[i+c] - 128))); }) },

        { id: 'flt-rgb-glitch', name: 'RGB Chromatic Glitch', cat: 'filters', desc: 'Horizontal signal chromatic shift', action: () =&gt; {
          const src = mainCtx.getImageData(0,0,CANVAS_WIDTH,CANVAS_HEIGHT);
          const dst = mainCtx.createImageData(CANVAS_WIDTH,CANVAS_HEIGHT);
          const s = src.data; const d = dst.data;
          d.set(s);
          const offset = 14;
          for(let y=0; y&lt;CANVAS_HEIGHT; y++) {
            for(let x=0; x&lt;CANVAS_WIDTH; x++) {
              const idx = (y*CANVAS_WIDTH + x)*4;
              const rIdx = (y*CANVAS_WIDTH + Math.min(CANVAS_WIDTH-1, x + offset))*4;
              const bIdx = (y*CANVAS_WIDTH + Math.max(0, x - offset))*4;
              d[idx] = s[rIdx];
              d[idx+2] = s[bIdx+2];
            }
          }
          mainCtx.putImageData(dst, 0, 0);
          saveHistorySnapshot();
        }},

        { id: 'flt-halftone', name: 'Halftone Comic Dots', cat: 'filters', desc: 'Pop art newsprint halftone matrix', action: () =&gt; {

          mainCtx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
          mainCtx.fillRect(0,0,CANVAS_WIDTH,CANVAS_HEIGHT);
          const step = 10;
          for(let y=0; y&lt;CANVAS_HEIGHT; y+=step) {
            for(let x=0; x&lt;CANVAS_WIDTH; x+=step) {
              const idx = (y*CANVAS_WIDTH + x)*4;
              const lum = 1 - (src.data[idx]*0.299 + src.data[idx+1]*0.587 + src.data[idx+2]*0.114)/255;
              const r = (step/2) * lum * 1.3;
              mainCtx.fillStyle = '#111827';
              mainCtx.beginPath();
              mainCtx.arc(x+step/2, y+step/2, r, 0, Math.PI*2);
              mainCtx.fill();
            }
          }
          saveHistorySnapshot();
        }},

        { id: 'tr-swirl', name: 'Twirl Vortex Center', cat: 'transform', desc: 'Vortical spiral displacement', action: () =&gt; {
          const src = mainCtx.getImageData(0,0,CANVAS_WIDTH,CANVAS_HEIGHT);
          const dst = mainCtx.createImageData(CANVAS_WIDTH,CANVAS_HEIGHT);
          const s = src.data; const d = dst.data;
          d.set(s);
          const cx = 400, cy = 400, maxR = 350, angle = 1.8;
          for(let y=0; y&lt;CANVAS_HEIGHT; y++) {
            for(let x=0; x&lt;CANVAS_WIDTH; x++) {
              const dx = x - cx; const dy = y - cy;
              const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
              if(dist &lt; maxR) {
                const a = Math.atan2(dy, dx) + angle * (1 - dist/maxR);
                const sx = Math.round(cx + Math.cos(a)*dist);
                const sy = Math.round(cy + Math.sin(a)*dist);
                if(sx &gt;= 0 &amp;&amp; sx &lt; CANVAS_WIDTH &amp;&amp; sy &gt;= 0 &amp;&amp; sy &lt; CANVAS_HEIGHT) {
                  const dIdx = (y*CANVAS_WIDTH + x)*4;
                  const sIdx = (sy*CANVAS_WIDTH + sx)*4;
                  for(let c=0; c&lt;4; c++) d[dIdx+c] = s[sIdx+c];
                }
              }
            }
          }
          mainCtx.putImageData(dst, 0, 0);
          saveHistorySnapshot();
        }},

        // 5. SHAPES &amp; FRAMES (~30)
        { id: 'shp-polaroid-frame', name: 'Polaroid Instant Frame', cat: 'shapes', desc: 'Add classic white photo card border', action: () =&gt; {
          mainCtx.save();
          mainCtx.lineWidth = 30;
          mainCtx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
          mainCtx.strokeRect(15, 15, CANVAS_WIDTH - 30, CANVAS_HEIGHT - 30);
          mainCtx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
          mainCtx.fillRect(0, CANVAS_HEIGHT - 120, CANVAS_WIDTH, 120);
          mainCtx.fillStyle = '#4b5563';
          mainCtx.font = 'italic bold 22px system-ui, sans-serif';
          mainCtx.textAlign = 'center';
          mainCtx.fillText('MINIEDIT MEMORIES', 400, CANVAS_HEIGHT - 45);
          mainCtx.restore();
          saveHistorySnapshot();
        }},
        { id: 'shp-neon-border', name: 'Cyber Neon Border', cat: 'shapes', desc: 'Glowing electric cyan frame', action: () =&gt; {
          mainCtx.save();
          mainCtx.shadowColor = '#06b6d4';
          mainCtx.shadowBlur = 25;
          mainCtx.strokeStyle = '#22d3ee';
          mainCtx.lineWidth = 10;
          mainCtx.strokeRect(20, 20, CANVAS_WIDTH - 40, CANVAS_HEIGHT - 40);
          mainCtx.restore();
          saveHistorySnapshot();
        }},
        { id: 'shp-golden-spiral', name: 'Golden Ratio Grid', cat: 'shapes', desc: 'Compositional rule of thirds overlay', action: () =&gt; {
          mainCtx.save();
          mainCtx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(250, 204, 21, 0.7)';
          mainCtx.lineWidth = 2;
          mainCtx.setLineDash([8, 6]);
          mainCtx.beginPath();
          mainCtx.moveTo(CANVAS_WIDTH/3, 0); mainCtx.lineTo(CANVAS_WIDTH/3, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
          mainCtx.moveTo(CANVAS_WIDTH*2/3, 0); mainCtx.lineTo(CANVAS_WIDTH*2/3, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
          mainCtx.moveTo(0, CANVAS_HEIGHT/3); mainCtx.lineTo(CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT/3);
          mainCtx.moveTo(0, CANVAS_HEIGHT*2/3); mainCtx.lineTo(CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT*2/3);
          mainCtx.stroke();
          mainCtx.restore();
          saveHistorySnapshot();
        }},
        { id: 'shp-badge-pro', name: 'Stamp PRO Verified Badge', cat: 'shapes', desc: 'Gold verified star badge in corner', action: () =&gt; {
          mainCtx.save();
          mainCtx.fillStyle = '#f59e0b';
          mainCtx.beginPath();
          mainCtx.arc(CANVAS_WIDTH - 70, 70, 36, 0, Math.PI*2);
          mainCtx.fill();
          mainCtx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
          mainCtx.font = 'bold 15px system-ui, sans-serif';
          mainCtx.textAlign = 'center';
          mainCtx.fillText('PRO', CANVAS_WIDTH - 70, 75);
          mainCtx.restore();
          saveHistorySnapshot();
        }},

        // 6. TYPOGRAPHY &amp; WATERMARKS (~17)
        { id: 'txt-bold-title', name: 'Add Hero Bold Title', cat: 'text', desc: 'Centered bold header text', action: () =&gt; {
          mainCtx.save();
          mainCtx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
          mainCtx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.8)';
          mainCtx.shadowBlur = 12;
          mainCtx.font = '900 48px system-ui, sans-serif';

          mainCtx.restore();
          saveHistorySnapshot();
        }},
        { id: 'txt-cyber-mono', name: 'Add Cyber Monospace Tag', cat: 'text', desc: 'Futuristic technical datestamp', action: () =&gt; {
          mainCtx.save();
          mainCtx.fillStyle = '#38bdf8';
          mainCtx.font = '700 18px monospace';
          mainCtx.textAlign = 'left';
          mainCtx.fillText('SYS.LOC // 800x800 // RENDERING ACTIVE', 30, CANVAS_HEIGHT - 30);
          mainCtx.restore();
          saveHistorySnapshot();
        }},
        { id: 'txt-meme-bottom', name: 'Add Meme Impact Caption', cat: 'text', desc: 'White impact meme banner text', action: () =&gt; {
          mainCtx.save();
          mainCtx.font = '900 44px Impact, sans-serif';
          mainCtx.textAlign = 'center';
          mainCtx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
          mainCtx.strokeStyle = '#000000';
