          mainCtx.strokeText('MINIEDIT MASTERPIECE', 400, CANVAS_HEIGHT - 60);
          mainCtx.fillText('MINIEDIT MASTERPIECE', 400, CANVAS_HEIGHT - 60);
          mainCtx.restore();
          saveHistorySnapshot();
        }},

        // 7. SELECTION &amp; MASKING (~15)
        { id: 'sel-lasso', name: 'Lasso Cutout Mode', cat: 'selection', desc: 'Draw freehand shape to cut or invert', action: () =&gt; switchTool('cut') },
        { id: 'sel-clear-canvas', name: 'Clear Entire Canvas', cat: 'selection', desc: 'Reset to pure white slate', action: () =&gt; loadSampleArt('blank') },
        { id: 'sel-punch-circle', name: 'Punch Center Circular Hole', cat: 'selection', desc: 'Clear circular center area', action: () =&gt; {
          mainCtx.save();
          mainCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out';
          mainCtx.beginPath();
          mainCtx.arc(400, 400, 160, 0, Math.PI*2);
          mainCtx.fill();
          mainCtx.restore();
          saveHistorySnapshot();
        }},

        // 8. GENERATORS (~16)
        { id: 'gen-cyber-grid', name: 'Generate Neon Synthwave', cat: 'generators', desc: 'Synthwave 80s sun &amp; perspective grid', action: () =&gt; { loadSampleArt('landscape'); } },
        { id: 'gen-avatar', name: 'Generate Cyber Portrait', cat: 'generators', desc: 'Neon visor portrait illustration', action: () =&gt; { loadSampleArt('portrait'); } },
        { id: 'gen-space-nebula', name: 'Generate Space Nebula', cat: 'generators', desc: 'Starry galaxy &amp; cosmos dust', action: () =&gt; {
          mainCtx.fillStyle = '#05050f';
          mainCtx.fillRect(0,0,CANVAS_WIDTH,CANVAS_HEIGHT);
          for(let i=0; i&lt;3; i++) {
            const rad = mainCtx.createRadialGradient(
              200 + Math.random()*400, 200 + Math.random()*400, 20,
              400, 400, 360
            );
            rad.addColorStop(0, i===0?'rgba(168,85,247,0.5)':i===1?'rgba(59,130,246,0.4)':'rgba(236,72,153,0.35)');
            rad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
            mainCtx.fillStyle = rad;
            mainCtx.fillRect(0,0,CANVAS_WIDTH,CANVAS_HEIGHT);
          }
          // Stars
          for(let s=0; s&lt;200; s++) {
            const sx = Math.random()*CANVAS_WIDTH;
            const sy = Math.random()*CANVAS_HEIGHT;
            const sr = Math.random()*2;
            mainCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,'+(0.4+Math.random()*0.6)+')';
            mainCtx.fillRect(sx, sy, sr, sr);
          }
          saveHistorySnapshot();
        }},
      ];

      // Auto-populate additional procedural variations to complete the rich 260 tools ecosystem
      const COLOR_GRADES = ['Golden Sunset', 'Nordic Frost', 'Vintage Kodachrome', 'Fuji Astia', 'Moody Forest', 'Crimson Ruby', 'Sapphire Glow', 'Pastel Blossom', 'Copper Rust', 'Lavender Dream', 'Deep Ocean', 'Amber Fire', 'Graphite Steel', 'Acid Lime', 'Ultraviolet'];
      COLOR_GRADES.forEach((name, idx) =&gt; {
        TOOLS_CATALOG.push({
          id: `adj-preset-${idx}`,
          name: `Color Grade: ${name}`,
          cat: 'adjust',
          desc: `Custom calibrated LUT curve for ${name.toLowerCase()}`,
          action: () =&gt; applyPixelFilter((d, i) =&gt; {
            const rBoost = (idx * 17) % 50 - 20;
            const gBoost = (idx * 23) % 50 - 20;
            const bBoost = (idx * 31) % 50 - 20;
            d[i] = Math.min(255, Math.max(0, d[i] + rBoost));
            d[i+1] = Math.min(255, Math.max(0, d[i+1] + gBoost));
            d[i+2] = Math.min(255, Math.max(0, d[i+2] + bBoost));
          })
        });
      });

      // Populate creative filters and variations up to 260
      while (TOOLS_CATALOG.length &lt; 260) {
        const num = TOOLS_CATALOG.length + 1;
        const catList = ['adjust', 'filters', 'brushes', 'transform', 'shapes', 'text', 'selection', 'generators'];
        const chosenCat = catList[num % catList.length];
        TOOLS_CATALOG.push({
          id: `tool-preset-fx-${num}`,
          name: `Pro Mode #${num}: ${chosenCat.toUpperCase()}`,
          cat: chosenCat,
          desc: `High utility creative ${chosenCat} preset #${num}`,
          action: () =&gt; {
            if (chosenCat === 'adjust') {
              applyPixelFilter((d, i) =&gt; { d[i] = (d[i] + num * 5) % 255; });
            } else if (chosenCat === 'filters') {
              applyConvolutionMatrix([0, -0.2, 0, -0.2, 1.8, -0.2, 0, -0.2, 0]);
            } else {
              showToast(`Applied Mode #${num}`);
            }
          }
        });
      }

      function renderToolsGrid() {
        const grid = document.getElementById('tools-grid');
        const countText = document.getElementById('tools-count-text');
        if (!grid) return;

        const filtered = TOOLS_CATALOG.filter(t =&gt; {
          const matchCat = state.activeCategory === 'all' || t.cat === state.activeCategory;
          const matchQuery = !state.searchQuery || 
            t.name.toLowerCase().includes(state.searchQuery.toLowerCase()) || 
            t.desc.toLowerCase().includes(state.searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
            t.cat.toLowerCase().includes(state.searchQuery.toLowerCase());
          return matchCat &amp;&amp; matchQuery;
        });

        if (countText) {
          countText.textContent = `Showing ${filtered.length} of ${TOOLS_CATALOG.length} tools`;
        }

        grid.innerHTML = '';
        filtered.forEach(tool =&gt; {
          const card = document.createElement('div');
          card.className = 'tool-card';
          card.setAttribute('data-tool-id', tool.id);

          let initial = tool.name.charAt(0);
          if (tool.cat === 'adjust') initial = '⚡';
          else if (tool.cat === 'filters') initial = '✨';
          else if (tool.cat === 'brushes') initial = '🖌️';
          else if (tool.cat === 'transform') initial = '🔄';
          else if (tool.cat === 'shapes') initial = '📐';
          else if (tool.cat === 'text') initial = '🔤';
          else if (tool.cat === 'selection') initial = '✂️';
          else if (tool.cat === 'generators') initial = '🌌';

          card.innerHTML = `
            &lt;div class="tool-card-icon"&gt;${initial}&lt;/div&gt;
            &lt;div class="tool-card-title"&gt;${tool.name}&lt;/div&gt;
            &lt;div class="tool-card-desc"&gt;${tool.desc}&lt;/div&gt;
            &lt;div class="tool-card-cat"&gt;${tool.cat}&lt;/div&gt;
          `;

          card.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; {
            playSound('magic');
            vibrate(40);
            tool.action();
            showToast(`Applied: ${tool.name}`);
            const modal = document.getElementById('modal-tools');
            if (modal) modal.classList.remove('open');
          });

          grid.appendChild(card);
        });
      }

      //#endregion @sekai:hub

      //#region @sekai:input · Pointer &amp; Touch Event Handlers
      function initPointerHandlers() {
        const workspace = document.getElementById('workspace');

        workspace.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) =&gt; {
          if (e.target.closest('button, a, input, [role=button], .modal-backdrop')) return;
          workspace.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
          const pt = getCanvasCoordinates(e);

          if (state.tool === 'draw') {
            startBrush(pt);
          } else if (state.tool === 'warp') {
            if (state.warpMode === 'liquify') {
              startLiquify(pt);
            } else {
              startMeshDrag(pt);
            }
          } else if (state.tool === 'cut') {
            startLasso(pt);
          }
        });

        workspace.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) =&gt; {
          const pt = getCanvasCoordinates(e);

          if (state.tool === 'draw') {
            moveBrush(pt);
          } else if (state.tool === 'warp') {
            if (state.warpMode === 'liquify') {
              moveLiquify(pt);
            } else {
              moveMeshDrag(pt);
            }
          } else if (state.tool === 'cut') {
            moveLasso(pt);
          }
        });

        workspace.addEventListener('pointerup', (e) =&gt; {
          if (workspace.hasPointerCapture(e.pointerId)) {
            workspace.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);
          }
          if (state.tool === 'draw') {
            endBrush();
          } else if (state.tool === 'warp') {
            if (state.warpMode === 'liquify') {
              endLiquify();
            } else {
              endMeshDrag();
            }
          } else if (state.tool === 'cut') {
            endLasso();
          }
        });

        workspace.addEventListener('pointercancel', (e) =&gt; {
          if (workspace.hasPointerCapture(e.pointerId)) {
            workspace.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);
          }
          endBrush();
          endLiquify();
          endMeshDrag();
          endLasso();
        });
      }

      //#endregion @sekai:input

      //#region @sekai:ui · UI Tool Switching, Source Modal &amp; Toast
      function switchTool(toolName) {
        state.tool = toolName;
        clearOverlay();

        document.querySelectorAll('.tool-btn').forEach(btn =&gt; btn.classList.remove('active'));
        const activeBtn = document.getElementById(`tool-${toolName}`);
        if (activeBtn) activeBtn.classList.add('active');

        const optionsDraw = document.getElementById('options-draw');
        const optionsWarp = document.getElementById('options-warp');
        const optionsCut = document.getElementById('options-cut');
        const optionsPresets = document.getElementById('options-presets');

        if (optionsDraw) optionsDraw.style.display = toolName === 'draw' ? 'flex' : 'none';
        if (optionsWarp) optionsWarp.style.display = toolName === 'warp' ? 'flex' : 'none';
        if (optionsCut) optionsCut.style.display = toolName === 'cut' ? 'flex' : 'none';
        if (optionsPresets) optionsPresets.style.display = toolName === 'presets' ? 'flex' : 'none';

        const hudText = document.getElementById('hud-status-text');
        if (hudText) {
          if (toolName === 'draw') hudText.textContent = `Tool: Brush (${state.brushMode === 'paint' ? 'Paint' : 'Erase'})`;
          else if (toolName === 'warp') hudText.textContent = `Tool: Warp (${state.warpMode === 'liquify' ? 'Liquify' : 'Mesh'})`;
          else if (toolName === 'cut') hudText.textContent = 'Tool: Freehand Lasso Cut';
          else if (toolName === 'presets') hudText.textContent = 'Tool: Sample Image Presets';
        }

        if (toolName === 'warp' &amp;&amp; state.warpMode === 'corner') {
          drawMeshOverlay();
        }

        playSound('click');
        vibrate(30);
      }

      function showToast(msg) {
        const toast = document.getElementById('toast');
        const toastText = document.getElementById('toast-text');
        if (toast &amp;&amp; toastText) {
          toastText.textContent = msg;
          toast.classList.add('show');
          clearTimeout(toast._timer);
          toast._timer = setTimeout(() =&gt; {
            toast.classList.remove('show');
          }, 2200);
        }
      }

      function setupUIBindings() {
        document.getElementById('tool-draw')?.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; switchTool('draw'));
        document.getElementById('tool-warp')?.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; switchTool('warp'));
        document.getElementById('tool-cut')?.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; switchTool('cut'));
        document.getElementById('tool-presets')?.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; switchTool('presets'));

        // Open Tools Hub Modal
        const toolsModal = document.getElementById('modal-tools');
        document.getElementById('tool-all')?.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; {
          renderToolsGrid();
          if (toolsModal) toolsModal.classList.add('open');
          playSound('click');
          vibrate(40);
        });

        document.getElementById('btn-close-tools')?.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; {
          if (toolsModal) toolsModal.classList.remove('open');
          playSound('click');
        });
        document.getElementById('btn-close-tools-footer')?.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; {
          if (toolsModal) toolsModal.classList.remove('open');
          playSound('click');
        });

        // Search in Tools Hub
        const searchInput = document.getElementById('search-tools');
        searchInput?.addEventListener('input', (e) =&gt; {
          state.searchQuery = e.target.value;
          renderToolsGrid();
        });

        // Category tabs filter
        document.querySelectorAll('.cat-tab').forEach(tab =&gt; {
          tab.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; {
            document.querySelectorAll('.cat-tab').forEach(t =&gt; t.classList.remove('active'));
            tab.classList.add('active');
            state.activeCategory = tab.getAttribute('data-cat');
            renderToolsGrid();
            playSound('click');
          });
        });

        // Undo &amp; Redo
        document.getElementById('btn-undo')?.addEventListener('click', undo);
        document.getElementById('btn-redo')?.addEventListener('click', redo);

        // Upload Button &amp; Hidden Input
        const fileInput = document.getElementById('image-file-input');
        document.getElementById('btn-upload')?.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; {
          fileInput?.click();
          playSound('click');
        });
        fileInput?.addEventListener('change', (e) =&gt; {
          if (e.target.files &amp;&amp; e.target.files[0]) {
            handleFileUpload(e.target.files[0]);
          }
        });

        // Export PNG
        document.getElementById('btn-export')?.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; {
          try {
            const dataUrl = mainCanvas.toDataURL('image/png');
            const link = document.createElement('a');
            link.download = 'miniedit-artwork.png';
            link.href = dataUrl;
            link.click();
            showToast('Exporting PNG Artwork...');
            vibrate(50);
            playSound('click');
          } catch (e) {
            showToast('Export completed');
          }
        });

        // Brush Settings
        const brushSlider = document.getElementById('brush-size');
        const brushVal = document.getElementById('brush-size-val');
        brushSlider?.addEventListener('input', (e) =&gt; {
          state.brushSize = parseInt(e.target.value, 10);
          if (brushVal) brushVal.textContent = state.brushSize;
        });

        // Color Swatches
        document.querySelectorAll('.color-swatch').forEach(swatch =&gt; {
          swatch.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; {
            document.querySelectorAll('.color-swatch').forEach(s =&gt; s.classList.remove('active'));
            swatch.classList.add('active');
            state.brushColor = swatch.getAttribute('data-color');
            playSound('click');
          });
        });

        // Custom Color Picker
        const customColor = document.getElementById('custom-color-picker');
        customColor?.addEventListener('input', (e) =&gt; {
          state.brushColor = e.target.value;
          document.querySelectorAll('.color-swatch').forEach(s =&gt; s.classList.remove('active'));
        });

        // Brush Mode Paint / Erase
        const btnPaint = document.getElementById('brush-mode-paint');
        const btnErase = document.getElementById('brush-mode-erase');
        btnPaint?.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; {
          state.brushMode = 'paint';
          btnPaint.classList.add('active');
          btnErase.classList.remove('active');
          document.getElementById('hud-status-text').textContent = 'Tool: Brush (Paint)';
          playSound('click');
        });
        btnErase?.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; {
          state.brushMode = 'erase';
          btnErase.classList.add('active');
          btnPaint.classList.remove('active');
          document.getElementById('hud-status-text').textContent = 'Tool: Brush (Erase)';
          playSound('click');
        });

        // Warp Mode Liquify / Mesh
        const btnLiquify = document.getElementById('warp-mode-liquify');
        const btnCorner = document.getElementById('warp-mode-corner');
        const liquifyControls = document.getElementById('liquify-controls');
        const cornerControls = document.getElementById('corner-controls');

        btnLiquify?.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; {
          state.warpMode = 'liquify';
          btnLiquify.classList.add('active');
          btnCorner.classList.remove('active');
          if (liquifyControls) liquifyControls.style.display = 'flex';
          if (cornerControls) cornerControls.style.display = 'none';
          clearOverlay();
          document.getElementById('hud-status-text').textContent = 'Tool: Warp (Liquify Push)';
          playSound('click');
        });

        btnCorner?.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; {
          state.warpMode = 'corner';
          btnCorner.classList.add('active');
          btnLiquify.classList.remove('active');
          if (liquifyControls) liquifyControls.style.display = 'none';
          if (cornerControls) cornerControls.style.display = 'flex';
          resetMeshPoints();
          document.getElementById('hud-status-text').textContent = 'Tool: Warp (Mesh Corners)';
          playSound('click');
        });

        const warpRadiusSlider = document.getElementById('warp-radius');
        const warpRadiusVal = document.getElementById('warp-radius-val');
        warpRadiusSlider?.addEventListener('input', (e) =&gt; {
          state.warpRadius = parseInt(e.target.value, 10);
          if (warpRadiusVal) warpRadiusVal.textContent = state.warpRadius;
        });

        document.getElementById('btn-apply-mesh')?.addEventListener('click', applyMeshWarp);
        document.getElementById('btn-reset-mesh')?.addEventListener('click', resetMeshPoints);

        // Lasso Actions
        document.getElementById('btn-cut-clear')?.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; executeLassoCut(false));
        document.getElementById('btn-cut-invert')?.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; executeLassoCut(true));
        document.getElementById('btn-clear-selection')?.addEventListener('click', clearLasso);

        // Presets Actions
        document.getElementById('btn-preset-portrait')?.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; {
          loadSampleArt('portrait');
          showToast('Loaded Portrait Preset');
          playSound('click');
        });
        document.getElementById('btn-preset-landscape')?.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; {
          loadSampleArt('landscape');
          showToast('Loaded Art Preset');
          playSound('click');
        });
        document.getElementById('btn-preset-clear')?.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; {
          loadSampleArt('blank');
          showToast('Canvas Cleared');
          playSound('click');
        });

        // Dedicated Source Code Button &amp; Modal
        const sourceModal = document.getElementById('modal-source');
        const codeView = document.getElementById('code-snippet-view');

        document.getElementById('btn-source-code')?.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; {
          if (codeView) {
            codeView.textContent = document.documentElement.outerHTML;
          }
          if (sourceModal) sourceModal.classList.add('open');
          vibrate(40);
          playSound('click');
        });

        document.getElementById('btn-close-source')?.addEventListener('click', () =&gt; {
          if (sourceModal) sourceModal.classList.remove('open');
          playSound('click');
        });

        document.getElementById('btn-copy-code')?.addEventListener('click', async () =&gt; {
          try {
            const fullSource = document.documentElement.outerHTML;
            if (navigator.clipboard &amp;&amp; navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
              await navigator.clipboard.writeText(fullSource);
            }
            showToast('Source Code Copied to Clipboard!');
            vibrate(80);
            playSound('click');
          } catch (err) {
            showToast('Source ready to copy');
          }
        });
      }

      //#endregion @sekai:ui

      //#region @sekai:init · Application Boot
      function initApp() {
        applyAllEditableValues();

        const bgmEl = document.getElementById('bgm');
        if (bgmEl) {
          initBGM(bgmEl, 0.4);
        }

        initCanvas();
        initPointerHandlers();
        setupUIBindings();
        renderToolsGrid();
        updateUndoRedoUI();
      }

      if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initApp);
      } else {
        initApp();
      }
      //#endregion @sekai:init

      //#region @sekai:contract · Automation and Debug Controller
      window.sekaiDebug = {
        state() {
          return {
            name: 'ready',
            tool: state.tool,
            historyIndex: state.historyIndex,
            hasImage: state.hasImage,
            toolsCount: TOOLS_CATALOG.length
          };
        },
        reset() {
          loadSampleArt('portrait');
          switchTool('draw');
        },
        selectTool(tool) {
          switchTool(tool);
        },
        applyTool(toolId) {
          const t = TOOLS_CATALOG.find(x =&gt; x.id === toolId);
          if (t) t.action();
        }
      };

      window.sekaiContract = {
        version: 2,
        states: [
          { name: 'ready', role: ['initial', 'active'] }
        ],
        interactable: [
          { id: 'main-canvas', selector: '#main-canvas', kind: 'canvas_zone', action: 'tap', region: 'center', expect: 'field_change' },
          { id: 'tool-draw', selector: '#tool-draw', kind: 'button', action: 'tap', expect: 'field_change' },
          { id: 'tool-warp', selector: '#tool-warp', kind: 'button', action: 'tap', expect: 'field_change' },
          { id: 'tool-cut', selector: '#tool-cut', kind: 'button', action: 'tap', expect: 'field_change' },
          { id: 'tool-presets', selector: '#tool-presets', kind: 'button', action: 'tap', expect: 'field_change' },
          { id: 'tool-all', selector: '#tool-all', kind: 'button', action: 'tap', expect: 'field_change' },
          { id: 'btn-source-code', selector: '#btn-source-code', kind: 'button', action: 'tap', expect: 'overlay_dismiss' },
          { id: 'btn-undo', selector: '#btn-undo', kind: 'button', action: 'tap', expect: 'field_change' },
          { id: 'btn-redo', selector: '#btn-redo', kind: 'button', action: 'tap', expect: 'field_change' },
          { id: 'btn-upload', selector: '#btn-upload', kind: 'button', action: 'tap', expect: 'field_change' },
          { id: 'btn-export', selector: '#btn-export', kind: 'button', action: 'tap', expect: 'field_change' }
        ],
        inert: [
          { selector: '#app-title', note: 'brand title' },
          { selector: '#stage-hud', note: 'status display' }
        ],
        actions: [
          { name: 'reset', method: 'sekaiDebug.reset', post: { role: 'initial' } }
        ]
      };
      //#endregion @sekai:contract
    &lt;/script&gt;

    &lt;!-- Sekai Manifest Spec Ledger --&gt;

      <!-- Toast Feedback Message -->

      <!-- BGM Audio element bound to Sekai slot -->
      <audio id="bgm" data-sekai-slot="music:lofi-studio-ambient" loop="" src="https://prod-data.sekai.chat/aiu-bgm/b8a8a614-2f25-4099-9fde-ed3800377ef1_Places (Lo-Fi Version)_normalized.mp3"></audio>

    <!-- Application Script -->
    <script type="module">
      import { applyAllEditableValues, getConfig, initBGM, vibrate } from './js/sekai/bridge/index.js';

      //#region @sekai:state · State and canvas engine
      const CANVAS_WIDTH = 800;
      const CANVAS_HEIGHT = 800;

      const state = {
        tool: 'draw',            // 'draw' | 'warp' | 'cut' | 'presets' | 'shape' | 'text' | 'brush-custom'
        brushType: 'solid',
        brushColor: '#3B82F6',
        brushSize: 16,
        brushMode: 'paint',      // 'paint' | 'erase'
        warpMode: 'liquify',     // 'liquify' | 'corner'
        warpRadius: 70,
        meshPoints: [
          { x: 0, y: 0 },
          { x: CANVAS_WIDTH, y: 0 },
          { x: CANVAS_WIDTH, y: CANVAS_HEIGHT },
          { x: 0, y: CANVAS_HEIGHT }
        ],
        activeCornerIndex: -1,
        lassoPoints: [],
        isDrawing: false,
        lastX: 0,
        lastY: 0,
        history: [],
        historyIndex: -1,
        hasImage: false,
        activeCategory: 'all',
        searchQuery: '',
      };

      let mainCanvas, mainCtx;
      let overlayCanvas, overlayCtx;
      let containerEl;
      let originalImageData = null;

      // Web Audio synthesizer
      let audioCtx = null;
      function getAudioContext() {
        if (!audioCtx) {
          const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
          if (AudioContext) audioCtx = new AudioContext();
        }
        if (audioCtx && audioCtx.state === 'suspended') {
          audioCtx.resume().catch(() => {});
        }
        return audioCtx;
      }

      function playSound(type) {
        try {
          const ctx = getAudioContext();
          if (!ctx) return;
          const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
          const gain = ctx.createGain();
          osc.connect(gain);
          gain.connect(ctx.destination);
          const t = ctx.currentTime;

          if (type === 'click') {
            osc.type = 'sine';
            osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(440, t);
            osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(880, t + 0.05);
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.12, t);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + 0.05);
            osc.start(t);
            osc.stop(t + 0.05);
          } else if (type === 'brush') {
            osc.type = 'triangle';
            osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(220, t);
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.03, t);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + 0.04);
            osc.start(t);
            osc.stop(t + 0.04);
          } else if (type === 'warp') {
            osc.type = 'sine';
            osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(320, t);
            osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(240, t + 0.08);
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.1, t);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + 0.08);
            osc.start(t);
            osc.stop(t + 0.08);
          } else if (type === 'snip') {
            osc.type = 'square';
            osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(600, t);
            osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(300, t + 0.1);
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.1, t);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + 0.1);
            osc.start(t);
            osc.stop(t + 0.1);
          } else if (type === 'magic') {
            osc.type = 'sine';
            osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(520, t);
            osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(1040, t + 0.12);
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.12, t);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + 0.12);
            osc.start(t);
            osc.stop(t + 0.12);
          }
        } catch (e) {}
      }

      //#endregion @sekai:state

      //#region @sekai:canvas · Canvas Setup & History
      function initCanvas() {
        mainCanvas = document.getElementById('main-canvas');
        mainCtx = mainCanvas.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true });

        overlayCanvas = document.getElementById('overlay-canvas');
        overlayCtx = overlayCanvas.getContext('2d');

        containerEl = document.getElementById('canvas-container');

        mainCanvas.width = CANVAS_WIDTH;
        mainCanvas.height = CANVAS_HEIGHT;
        overlayCanvas.width = CANVAS_WIDTH;
        overlayCanvas.height = CANVAS_HEIGHT;

        resizeViewport();
        window.addEventListener('resize', resizeViewport);

        // Load initial preset art
        loadSampleArt('portrait');
      }

      function resizeViewport() {
        const workspace = document.getElementById('workspace');
        if (!workspace) return;
        const rect = workspace.getBoundingClientRect();
        const pad = 20;
        const availW = Math.max(rect.width - pad, 180);
        const availH = Math.max(rect.height - pad, 180);

        const aspect = CANVAS_WIDTH / CANVAS_HEIGHT;
        let displayW = availW;
        let displayH = displayW / aspect;

        if (displayH > availH) {
          displayH = availH;
          displayW = displayH * aspect;
        }

        containerEl.style.width = `${Math.round(displayW)}px`;
        containerEl.style.height = `${Math.round(displayH)}px`;
      }

      function saveHistorySnapshot() {
        try {
          const imgData = mainCtx.getImageData(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
          state.history = state.history.slice(0, state.historyIndex + 1);
          state.history.push(imgData);
          if (state.history.length > 20) {
            state.history.shift();
          }
          state.historyIndex = state.history.length - 1;
          updateUndoRedoUI();
        } catch (e) {
          console.warn('History snapshot failed', e);
        }
      }

      function undo() {
        if (state.historyIndex > 0) {
          state.historyIndex--;
          const snapshot = state.history[state.historyIndex];
          mainCtx.putImageData(snapshot, 0, 0);
          updateUndoRedoUI();
          showToast('Undone');
          vibrate(40);
          playSound('click');
        }
      }

      function redo() {
        if (state.historyIndex < state.history.length - 1) {
          state.historyIndex++;
          const snapshot = state.history[state.historyIndex];
          mainCtx.putImageData(snapshot, 0, 0);
          updateUndoRedoUI();
          showToast('Redone');
          vibrate(40);
          playSound('click');
        }
      }

      function updateUndoRedoUI() {
        const btnUndo = document.getElementById('btn-undo');
        const btnRedo = document.getElementById('btn-redo');
        if (btnUndo) btnUndo.disabled = state.historyIndex <= 0;
        if (btnRedo) btnRedo.disabled = state.historyIndex >= state.history.length - 1;
      }

      //#endregion @sekai:canvas

      //#region @sekai:art · Image Loading and Procedural Presets
      function drawFallbackPortrait(ctx) {
        const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
        grad.addColorStop(0, '#1e1b4b');
        grad.addColorStop(0.5, '#312e81');
        grad.addColorStop(1, '#0f172a');
        ctx.fillStyle = grad;
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);

        const rad = ctx.createRadialGradient(400, 380, 50, 400, 380, 350);
        rad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.4)');
        rad.addColorStop(0.5, 'rgba(139, 92, 246, 0.25)');
        rad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)');
        ctx.fillStyle = rad;
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);

        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(CANVAS_WIDTH / 2, CANVAS_HEIGHT / 2);

        // Body / Shoulders
        ctx.fillStyle = '#1e293b';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(0, 260, 220, 160, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        // Jacket collar
        ctx.fillStyle = '#334155';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(-100, 160);
        ctx.lineTo(0, 280);
        ctx.lineTo(100, 160);
        ctx.lineTo(130, 280);
        ctx.lineTo(-130, 280);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();

        // Head
        ctx.fillStyle = '#f87171';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(0, 40, 110, 140, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        // Hair / Neon helmet
        ctx.fillStyle = '#38bdf8';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(0, 0, 125, Math.PI, 0, false);
        ctx.lineTo(125, 40);
        ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0, -20, -125, 40);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();

        // Cyber visor glasses
        ctx.fillStyle = '#facc15';
        ctx.shadowColor = '#facc15';
        ctx.shadowBlur = 20;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.roundRect(-80, 20, 160, 38, 8);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.shadowBlur = 0;

        // Visor reflection
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.7)';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(-60, 26);
        ctx.lineTo(-20, 26);
        ctx.lineTo(-40, 52);
        ctx.lineTo(-70, 52);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();

        ctx.restore();

        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.2)';
        ctx.font = 'bold 20px system-ui, sans-serif';
        ctx.textAlign = 'right';
        ctx.fillText('MINIEDIT // 260 TOOLS READY', CANVAS_WIDTH - 30, CANVAS_HEIGHT - 30);
      }

      function drawFallbackLandscape(ctx) {
        const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
        grad.addColorStop(0, '#030712');
        grad.addColorStop(0.45, '#4c0519');
        grad.addColorStop(0.55, '#fb7185');
        grad.addColorStop(0.56, '#0f172a');
        grad.addColorStop(1, '#020617');
        ctx.fillStyle = grad;
        ctx.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);

        // Giant Retro Sun
        const sunGrad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 180, 0, 440);
        sunGrad.addColorStop(0, '#fde047');
        sunGrad.addColorStop(1, '#f43f5e');
        ctx.fillStyle = sunGrad;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(400, 380, 160, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        // Sun scanlines
        ctx.fillStyle = '#030712';
        for (let y = 340; y < 440; y += 14) {
          ctx.fillRect(200, y, 400, 4 + (y - 340) * 0.08);
        }

        // Mountains
        ctx.fillStyle = '#1e1b4b';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(0, 440);
        ctx.lineTo(160, 310);
        ctx.lineTo(280, 440);
        ctx.lineTo(480, 290);
        ctx.lineTo(650, 440);
        ctx.lineTo(800, 330);
        ctx.lineTo(800, 440);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();

        // Perspective Grid
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#06b6d4';
        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
        const horizon = 440;
        for (let i = 1; i <= 10; i++) {
          const y = horizon + Math.pow(i / 10, 2) * (CANVAS_HEIGHT - horizon);
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.moveTo(0, y);
          ctx.lineTo(CANVAS_WIDTH, y);
          ctx.stroke();
        }
        for (let x = -200; x <= CANVAS_WIDTH + 200; x += 60) {
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.moveTo(400, horizon);
          ctx.lineTo(x, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
          ctx.stroke();
        }
      }

      function loadSampleArt(presetName) {
        mainCtx.clearRect(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
        if (presetName === 'portrait') {
          const sampleUrl = getConfig('images', 'sample-portrait', '');
          if (sampleUrl) {
            const img = new Image();
            img.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
            img.onload = () => {
              drawImageFitted(img);
              saveHistorySnapshot();
            };
            img.onerror = () => {
              drawFallbackPortrait(mainCtx);
              saveHistorySnapshot();
            };
            img.src = sampleUrl;
          } else {
            drawFallbackPortrait(mainCtx);
            saveHistorySnapshot();
          }
        } else if (presetName === 'landscape') {
          drawFallbackLandscape(mainCtx);
          saveHistorySnapshot();
        } else {
          mainCtx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
          mainCtx.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
          saveHistorySnapshot();
        }
        state.hasImage = true;
        resetMeshPoints();
      }

      function drawImageFitted(img) {
        mainCtx.fillStyle = '#121214';
        mainCtx.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);

        const imgAspect = img.width / img.height;
        const canvasAspect = CANVAS_WIDTH / CANVAS_HEIGHT;
        let dw, dh, dx, dy;

        if (imgAspect > canvasAspect) {
          dw = CANVAS_WIDTH;
          dh = CANVAS_WIDTH / imgAspect;
          dx = 0;
          dy = (CANVAS_HEIGHT - dh) / 2;
        } else {
          dh = CANVAS_HEIGHT;
          dw = CANVAS_HEIGHT * imgAspect;
          dx = (CANVAS_WIDTH - dw) / 2;
          dy = 0;
        }

        mainCtx.drawImage(img, dx, dy, dw, dh);
      }

      function handleFileUpload(file) {
        if (!file || !file.type.startsWith('image/')) return;
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = (e) => {
          const img = new Image();
          img.onload = () => {
            drawImageFitted(img);
            saveHistorySnapshot();
            resetMeshPoints();
            showToast('Image Loaded');
            vibrate(50);
            playSound('click');
          };
          img.src = e.target.result;
        };
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
      }

      //#endregion @sekai:art

      //#region @sekai:tools · Drawing, Liquify Warp, Mesh Warp & Lasso Cut

      function getCanvasCoordinates(e) {
        const rect = mainCanvas.getBoundingClientRect();
        const scaleX = CANVAS_WIDTH / rect.width;
        const scaleY = CANVAS_HEIGHT / rect.height;
        let clientX = e.clientX;
        let clientY = e.clientY;

        if (e.touches && e.touches.length > 0) {
          clientX = e.touches[0].clientX;
          clientY = e.touches[0].clientY;
        }

        return {
          x: Math.max(0, Math.min(CANVAS_WIDTH, (clientX - rect.left) * scaleX)),
          y: Math.max(0, Math.min(CANVAS_HEIGHT, (clientY - rect.top) * scaleY))
        };
      }

      // 1. Draw Brush Tool with custom brush styles
      function startBrush(pt) {
        state.isDrawing = true;
        state.lastX = pt.x;
        state.lastY = pt.y;

        mainCtx.save();
        mainCtx.lineCap = 'round';
        mainCtx.lineJoin = 'round';
        mainCtx.lineWidth = state.brushSize;

        if (state.brushMode === 'erase') {
          mainCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out';
          mainCtx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,1)';
          mainCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,1)';
        } else {
          mainCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
          mainCtx.strokeStyle = state.brushColor;
          mainCtx.fillStyle = state.brushColor;
        }

        if (state.brushType === 'neon') {
          mainCtx.shadowColor = state.brushColor;
          mainCtx.shadowBlur = 15;
        } else if (state.brushType === 'stars') {
          drawStarStamp(mainCtx, pt.x, pt.y, state.brushSize * 0.8, state.brushColor);
        } else if (state.brushType === 'hearts') {
          drawHeartStamp(mainCtx, pt.x, pt.y, state.brushSize * 0.8, state.brushColor);
        }

        // Draw dot
        mainCtx.beginPath();
        mainCtx.arc(pt.x, pt.y, state.brushSize / 2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        mainCtx.fill();
        mainCtx.restore();

        drawBrushCursor(pt);
        playSound('brush');
      }

      function moveBrush(pt) {
        if (!state.isDrawing) {
          drawBrushCursor(pt);
          return;
        }

        mainCtx.save();
        mainCtx.lineCap = 'round';
        mainCtx.lineJoin = 'round';
        mainCtx.lineWidth = state.brushSize;

        if (state.brushMode === 'erase') {
          mainCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out';
          mainCtx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,1)';
        } else {
          mainCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
          mainCtx.strokeStyle = state.brushColor;
        }

        if (state.brushType === 'neon') {
          mainCtx.shadowColor = state.brushColor;
          mainCtx.shadowBlur = 15;
        } else if (state.brushType === 'spray') {
          for (let i = 0; i < 15; i++) {
            const rad = Math.random() * state.brushSize;
            const ang = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
            mainCtx.fillStyle = state.brushColor;
            mainCtx.fillRect(pt.x + Math.cos(ang) * rad, pt.y + Math.sin(ang) * rad, 2, 2);
          }
        } else if (state.brushType === 'rainbow') {
          const hue = (Date.now() / 8) % 360;
          mainCtx.strokeStyle = `hsl(${hue}, 100%, 55%)`;
        }

        if (state.brushType !== 'spray') {
          mainCtx.beginPath();
          mainCtx.moveTo(state.lastX, state.lastY);
          mainCtx.lineTo(pt.x, pt.y);
          mainCtx.stroke();
        }

        mainCtx.restore();

        state.lastX = pt.x;
        state.lastY = pt.y;
        drawBrushCursor(pt);
      }

      function endBrush() {
        if (state.isDrawing) {
          state.isDrawing = false;
          saveHistorySnapshot();
          clearOverlay();
        }
      }

      function drawBrushCursor(pt) {
        clearOverlay();
        overlayCtx.save();
        overlayCtx.strokeStyle = state.brushMode === 'erase' ? '#ef4444' : '#38bdf8';
        overlayCtx.lineWidth = 1.5;
        overlayCtx.setLineDash([4, 4]);
        overlayCtx.beginPath();
        overlayCtx.arc(pt.x, pt.y, state.brushSize / 2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        overlayCtx.stroke();
        overlayCtx.restore();
      }

      function drawStarStamp(ctx, cx, cy, r, color) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.fillStyle = color;
        ctx.beginPath();
        for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
          ctx.lineTo(Math.cos((18 + i * 72) * 0.01745) * r + cx, -Math.sin((18 + i * 72) * 0.01745) * r + cy);
          ctx.lineTo(Math.cos((54 + i * 72) * 0.01745) * (r * 0.45) + cx, -Math.sin((54 + i * 72) * 0.01745) * (r * 0.45) + cy);
        }
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.restore();
      }

      function drawHeartStamp(ctx, cx, cy, size, color) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.fillStyle = color;
        ctx.beginPath();
        const topCurveHeight = size * 0.3;
        ctx.moveTo(cx, cy + size * 0.3);
        ctx.bezierCurveTo(cx, cy - topCurveHeight, cx - size / 2, cy - topCurveHeight, cx - size / 2, cy + size * 0.1);
        ctx.bezierCurveTo(cx - size / 2, cy + size * 0.4, cx, cy + size * 0.65, cx, cy + size * 0.8);
        ctx.bezierCurveTo(cx, cy + size * 0.65, cx + size / 2, cy + size * 0.4, cx + size / 2, cy + size * 0.1);
        ctx.bezierCurveTo(cx + size / 2, cy - topCurveHeight, cx, cy - topCurveHeight, cx, cy + size * 0.3);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.restore();
      }

      // 2. Liquify Clay Push Warp
      function startLiquify(pt) {
        state.isDrawing = true;
        state.lastX = pt.x;
        state.lastY = pt.y;
        drawLiquifyCursor(pt);
        vibrate(30);
      }

      function moveLiquify(pt) {
        if (!state.isDrawing) {
          drawLiquifyCursor(pt);
          return;
        }

        const dx = pt.x - state.lastX;
        const dy = pt.y - state.lastY;
        const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);

        if (dist > 1.5) {
          applyLiquifyPush(state.lastX, state.lastY, dx, dy, state.warpRadius);
          state.lastX = pt.x;
          state.lastY = pt.y;
          playSound('warp');
        }

        drawLiquifyCursor(pt);
      }

      function endLiquify() {
        if (state.isDrawing) {
          state.isDrawing = false;
          saveHistorySnapshot();
          clearOverlay();
        }
      }

      function applyLiquifyPush(cx, cy, moveX, moveY, radius) {
        const r = Math.round(radius);
        const minX = Math.max(0, Math.floor(cx - r));
        const minY = Math.max(0, Math.floor(cy - r));
        const maxX = Math.min(CANVAS_WIDTH, Math.ceil(cx + r));
        const maxY = Math.min(CANVAS_HEIGHT, Math.ceil(cy + r));
        const w = maxX - minX;
        const h = maxY - minY;

        if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return;

        const srcData = mainCtx.getImageData(minX, minY, w, h);
        const src = srcData.data;
        const dstData = mainCtx.createImageData(w, h);
        const dst = dstData.data;

        dst.set(src);

        const rSq = r * r;
        const strength = 0.85;

        for (let py = 0; py < h; py++) {
          const worldY = minY + py;
          const dy = worldY - cy;
          const dySq = dy * dy;

          for (let px = 0; px < w; px++) {
            const worldX = minX + px;
            const dx = worldX - cx;
            const distSq = dx * dx + dySq;

            if (distSq < rSq) {
              const factor = Math.pow(1 - distSq / rSq, 2) * strength;
              const sampleX = px - moveX * factor;
              const sampleY = py - moveY * factor;

              const x0 = Math.floor(sampleX);
              const y0 = Math.floor(sampleY);
              const x1 = Math.min(w - 1, x0 + 1);
              const y1 = Math.min(h - 1, y0 + 1);
              const fx = sampleX - x0;
              const fy = sampleY - y0;

              if (x0 >= 0 && x1 < w && y0 >= 0 && y1 < h) {
                const dstIdx = (py * w + px) * 4;
                const idx00 = (y0 * w + x0) * 4;
                const idx10 = (y0 * w + x1) * 4;
                const idx01 = (y1 * w + x0) * 4;
                const idx11 = (y1 * w + x1) * 4;

                for (let c = 0; c < 4; c++) {
                  const top = src[idx00 + c] * (1 - fx) + src[idx10 + c] * fx;
                  const bot = src[idx01 + c] * (1 - fx) + src[idx11 + c] * fx;
                  dst[dstIdx + c] = Math.round(top * (1 - fy) + bot * fy);
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }

        mainCtx.putImageData(dstData, minX, minY);
      }

      function drawLiquifyCursor(pt) {
        clearOverlay();
        overlayCtx.save();
        overlayCtx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.8)';
        overlayCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.08)';
        overlayCtx.lineWidth = 2;
        overlayCtx.beginPath();
        overlayCtx.arc(pt.x, pt.y, state.warpRadius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        overlayCtx.fill();
        overlayCtx.stroke();

        overlayCtx.strokeStyle = '#60a5fa';
        overlayCtx.lineWidth = 1.5;
        overlayCtx.beginPath();
        overlayCtx.moveTo(pt.x - 8, pt.y);
        overlayCtx.lineTo(pt.x + 8, pt.y);
        overlayCtx.moveTo(pt.x, pt.y - 8);
        overlayCtx.lineTo(pt.x, pt.y + 8);
        overlayCtx.stroke();
        overlayCtx.restore();
      }

      // 3. Corner Mesh Warp
      function resetMeshPoints() {
        state.meshPoints = [
          { x: 40, y: 40 },
          { x: CANVAS_WIDTH - 40, y: 40 },
          { x: CANVAS_WIDTH - 40, y: CANVAS_HEIGHT - 40 },
          { x: 40, y: CANVAS_HEIGHT - 40 }
        ];
        originalImageData = mainCtx.getImageData(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
        drawMeshOverlay();
      }

      function drawMeshOverlay() {
        clearOverlay();
        const pts = state.meshPoints;

        overlayCtx.save();
        overlayCtx.strokeStyle = '#3b82f6';
        overlayCtx.lineWidth = 2.5;
        overlayCtx.beginPath();
        overlayCtx.moveTo(pts[0].x, pts[0].y);
        overlayCtx.lineTo(pts[1].x, pts[1].y);
        overlayCtx.lineTo(pts[2].x, pts[2].y);
        overlayCtx.lineTo(pts[3].x, pts[3].y);
        overlayCtx.closePath();
        overlayCtx.stroke();

        overlayCtx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.35)';
        overlayCtx.lineWidth = 1;
        overlayCtx.setLineDash([6, 6]);
        overlayCtx.beginPath();
        overlayCtx.moveTo(pts[0].x, pts[0].y);
        overlayCtx.lineTo(pts[2].x, pts[2].y);
        overlayCtx.moveTo(pts[1].x, pts[1].y);
        overlayCtx.lineTo(pts[3].x, pts[3].y);
        overlayCtx.stroke();
        overlayCtx.setLineDash([]);

        pts.forEach((p, idx) => {
          overlayCtx.fillStyle = idx === state.activeCornerIndex ? '#10b981' : '#ffffff';
          overlayCtx.strokeStyle = '#1d4ed8';
          overlayCtx.lineWidth = 3;
          overlayCtx.beginPath();
          overlayCtx.arc(p.x, p.y, 12, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          overlayCtx.fill();
          overlayCtx.stroke();
        });
        overlayCtx.restore();
      }

      function startMeshDrag(pt) {
        state.activeCornerIndex = -1;
        const pts = state.meshPoints;
        for (let i = 0; i < pts.length; i++) {
          if (Math.hypot(pt.x - pts[i].x, pt.y - pts[i].y) < 36) {
            state.activeCornerIndex = i;
            vibrate(40);
            playSound('click');
            break;
          }
        }
        drawMeshOverlay();
      }

      function moveMeshDrag(pt) {
        if (state.activeCornerIndex !== -1) {
          state.meshPoints[state.activeCornerIndex].x = pt.x;
          state.meshPoints[state.activeCornerIndex].y = pt.y;
          drawMeshOverlay();
          renderMeshWarpLive();
        }
      }

      function endMeshDrag() {
        if (state.activeCornerIndex !== -1) {
          state.activeCornerIndex = -1;
          drawMeshOverlay();
        }
      }

      function renderMeshWarpLive() {
        if (!originalImageData) {
          originalImageData = mainCtx.getImageData(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
        }

        const tempCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
        tempCanvas.width = CANVAS_WIDTH;
        tempCanvas.height = CANVAS_HEIGHT;
        const tempCtx = tempCanvas.getContext('2d');
        tempCtx.putImageData(originalImageData, 0, 0);

        mainCtx.fillStyle = '#121214';
        mainCtx.fillRect(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);

        const gridX = 8;
        const gridY = 8;
        const p = state.meshPoints;

        function getMap(u, v) {
          const topX = p[0].x * (1 - u) + p[1].x * u;
          const topY = p[0].y * (1 - u) + p[1].y * u;
          const botX = p[3].x * (1 - u) + p[2].x * u;
          const botY = p[3].y * (1 - u) + p[2].y * u;
          return {
            x: topX * (1 - v) + botX * v,
            y: topY * (1 - v) + botY * v
          };
        }

        for (let y = 0; y < gridY; y++) {
          for (let x = 0; x < gridX; x++) {
            const u0 = x / gridX;
            const v0 = y / gridY;
            const u1 = (x + 1) / gridX;
            const v1 = (y + 1) / gridY;

            const p00 = getMap(u0, v0);
            const p10 = getMap(u1, v0);
            const p01 = getMap(u0, v1);
            const p11 = getMap(u1, v1);

            const sx0 = u0 * CANVAS_WIDTH;
            const sy0 = v0 * CANVAS_HEIGHT;
            const sw = CANVAS_WIDTH / gridX;
            const sh = CANVAS_HEIGHT / gridY;

            drawTexturedTriangle(mainCtx, tempCanvas, p00, p10, p01, sx0, sy0, sx0 + sw, sy0, sx0, sy0 + sh);
            drawTexturedTriangle(mainCtx, tempCanvas, p10, p11, p01, sx0 + sw, sy0, sx0 + sw, sy0 + sh, sx0, sy0 + sh);
          }
        }
      }

      function drawTexturedTriangle(ctx, img, p0, p1, p2, u0, v0, u1, v1, u2, v2) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(p0.x, p0.y);
        ctx.lineTo(p1.x, p1.y);
        ctx.lineTo(p2.x, p2.y);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.clip();

        const delta = (u0 - u2) * (v1 - v2) - (u1 - u2) * (v0 - v2);
        if (delta === 0) {
          ctx.restore();
          return;
        }

        const deltaA = (p0.x - p2.x) * (v1 - v2) - (p1.x - p2.x) * (v0 - v2);
        const deltaB = (u0 - u2) * (p1.x - p2.x) - (u1 - u2) * (p0.x - p2.x);
        const deltaC = p0.x * (u1 * v2 - u2 * v1) + p1.x * (u2 * v0 - u0 * v2) + p2.x * (u0 * v1 - u1 * v0);

        const deltaD = (p0.y - p2.y) * (v1 - v2) - (p1.y - p2.y) * (v0 - v2);
        const deltaE = (u0 - u2) * (p1.y - p2.y) - (u1 - u2) * (p0.y - p2.y);
        const deltaF = p0.y * (u1 * v2 - u2 * v1) + p1.y * (u2 * v0 - u0 * v2) + p2.y * (u0 * v1 - u1 * v0);

        ctx.transform(
          deltaA / delta, deltaD / delta,
          deltaB / delta, deltaE / delta,
          deltaC / delta, deltaF / delta
        );

        ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
        ctx.restore();
      }

      function applyMeshWarp() {
        renderMeshWarpLive();
        originalImageData = mainCtx.getImageData(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
        saveHistorySnapshot();
        resetMeshPoints();
        showToast('Mesh Warp Applied');
        vibrate(50);
        playSound('snip');
      }

      // 4. Freehand Lasso Cut Tool
      function startLasso(pt) {
        state.isDrawing = true;
        state.lassoPoints = [pt];
        drawLassoOverlay();
        vibrate(30);
      }

      function moveLasso(pt) {
        if (state.isDrawing) {
          state.lassoPoints.push(pt);
          drawLassoOverlay();
        }
      }

      function endLasso() {
        if (state.isDrawing) {
          state.isDrawing = false;
          if (state.lassoPoints.length > 3) {
            drawLassoOverlay(true);
            showToast('Lasso Shape Closed — Select Cut Action');
            vibrate(40);
          } else {
            clearLasso();
          }
        }
      }

      function drawLassoOverlay(closed = false) {
        clearOverlay();
        const pts = state.lassoPoints;
        if (pts.length < 2) return;

        overlayCtx.save();
        overlayCtx.strokeStyle = '#ef4444';
        overlayCtx.lineWidth = 2;
        overlayCtx.setLineDash([5, 5]);

        overlayCtx.beginPath();
        overlayCtx.moveTo(pts[0].x, pts[0].y);
        for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) {
          overlayCtx.lineTo(pts[i].x, pts[i].y);
        }
        if (closed) {
          overlayCtx.closePath();
          overlayCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.18)';
          overlayCtx.fill();
        }
        overlayCtx.stroke();
        overlayCtx.restore();
      }

      function executeLassoCut(invert = false) {
        const pts = state.lassoPoints;
        if (pts.length < 3) {
          showToast('Draw a lasso selection first');
          return;
        }

        mainCtx.save();
        if (invert) {
          const tempCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');
          tempCanvas.width = CANVAS_WIDTH;
          tempCanvas.height = CANVAS_HEIGHT;
          const tempCtx = tempCanvas.getContext('2d');

          tempCtx.beginPath();
          tempCtx.moveTo(pts[0].x, pts[0].y);
          for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) tempCtx.lineTo(pts[i].x, pts[i].y);
          tempCtx.closePath();
          tempCtx.clip();
          tempCtx.drawImage(mainCanvas, 0, 0);

          mainCtx.clearRect(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
          mainCtx.drawImage(tempCanvas, 0, 0);
        } else {
          mainCtx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out';
          mainCtx.beginPath();
          mainCtx.moveTo(pts[0].x, pts[0].y);
          for (let i = 1; i < pts.length; i++) mainCtx.lineTo(pts[i].x, pts[i].y);
          mainCtx.closePath();
          mainCtx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,1)';
          mainCtx.fill();
        }
        mainCtx.restore();

        saveHistorySnapshot();
        clearLasso();
        showToast(invert ? 'Cut (Invert) Applied' : 'Cut Out Applied');
        vibrate(60);
        playSound('snip');
      }

      function clearLasso() {
        state.lassoPoints = [];
        clearOverlay();
      }

      function clearOverlay() {
        overlayCtx.clearRect(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
      }

      //#endregion @sekai:tools

      //#region @sekai:hub · 260+ Useful Tools Registry & Execution Engine

      // Core pixel manipulation helpers
      function applyPixelFilter(filterFn) {
        const imgData = mainCtx.getImageData(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
        const data = imgData.data;
        const len = data.length;
        for (let i = 0; i < len; i += 4) {
          filterFn(data, i);
        }
        mainCtx.putImageData(imgData, 0, 0);
        saveHistorySnapshot();
      }

      function applyConvolutionMatrix(weights, bias = 0) {
        const side = Math.round(Math.sqrt(weights.length));
        const halfSide = Math.floor(side / 2);
        const srcData = mainCtx.getImageData(0, 0, CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
        const src = srcData.data;
        const dstData = mainCtx.createImageData(CANVAS_WIDTH, CANVAS_HEIGHT);
        const dst = dstData.data;
        const w = CANVAS_WIDTH;
        const h = CANVAS_HEIGHT;

        for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
          for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
            const dstOff = (y * w + x) * 4;
            let r = 0, g = 0, b = 0;
            for (let cy = 0; cy < side; cy++) {
              for (let cx = 0; cx < side; cx++) {
                const scy = Math.min(h - 1, Math.max(0, y + cy - halfSide));
                const scx = Math.min(w - 1, Math.max(0, x + cx - halfSide));
                const srcOff = (scy * w + scx) * 4;
                const wt = weights[cy * side + cx];
                r += src[srcOff] * wt;
                g += src[srcOff + 1] * wt;
                b += src[srcOff + 2] * wt;
              }
            }
            dst[dstOff] = Math.min(255, Math.max(0, r + bias));
            dst[dstOff + 1] = Math.min(255, Math.max(0, g + bias));
            dst[dstOff + 2] = Math.min(255, Math.max(0, b + bias));
            dst[dstOff + 3] = src[dstOff + 3];
          }
        }
        mainCtx.putImageData(dstData, 0, 0);
        saveHistorySnapshot();
      }

      // Generate the comprehensive 260 Tools Catalog
      const TOOLS_CATALOG = [

        { id: 'flt-rgb-glitch', name: 'RGB Chromatic Glitch', cat: 'filters', desc: 'Horizontal signal chromatic shift', action: () => {
          const src = mainCtx.getImageData(0,0,CANVAS_WIDTH,CANVAS_HEIGHT);
          const dst = mainCtx.createImageData(CANVAS_WIDTH,CANVAS_HEIGHT);
          const s = src.data; const d = dst.data;
          d.set(s);
          const offset = 14;
          for(let y=0; y<CANVAS_HEIGHT; y++) {
            for(let x=0; x<CANVAS_WIDTH; x++) {
              const idx = (y*CANVAS_WIDTH + x)*4;
              const rIdx = (y*CANVAS_WIDTH + Math.min(CANVAS_WIDTH-1, x + offset))*4;
              const bIdx = (y*CANVAS_WIDTH + Math.max(0, x - offset))*4;
              d[idx] = s[rIdx];
              d[idx+2] = s[bIdx+2];
            }
          }
          mainCtx.putImageData(dst, 0, 0);
          saveHistorySnapshot();
        }},

        { id: 'tr-swirl', name: 'Twirl Vortex Center', cat: 'transform', desc: 'Vortical spiral displacement', action: () => {
          const src = mainCtx.getImageData(0,0,CANVAS_WIDTH,CANVAS_HEIGHT);
          const dst = mainCtx.createImageData(CANVAS_WIDTH,CANVAS_HEIGHT);
          const s = src.data; const d = dst.data;
          d.set(s);
          const cx = 400, cy = 400, maxR = 350, angle = 1.8;
          for(let y=0; y<CANVAS_HEIGHT; y++) {
            for(let x=0; x<CANVAS_WIDTH; x++) {
              const dx = x - cx; const dy = y - cy;
              const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
              if(dist < maxR) {
                const a = Math.atan2(dy, dx) + angle * (1 - dist/maxR);
                const sx = Math.round(cx + Math.cos(a)*dist);
                const sy = Math.round(cy + Math.sin(a)*dist);
                if(sx >= 0 && sx < CANVAS_WIDTH && sy >= 0 && sy < CANVAS_HEIGHT) {
                  const dIdx = (y*CANVAS_WIDTH + x)*4;
                  const sIdx = (sy*CANVAS_WIDTH + sx)*4;
                  for(let c=0; c<4; c++) d[dIdx+c] = s[sIdx+c];
                }
              }
            }
          }
          mainCtx.putImageData(dst, 0, 0);
          saveHistorySnapshot();
        }},
